  it.c2 = paint([255, 255, 255], 1, 0, HALF.d, alpha * .38);
  it.hl = hl;
  return it;
}
function pushBonds(x0, y0, x1, y1, count, d, alpha, s){
  const it = item(2, d);
  it.x0 = x0; it.y0 = y0; it.x1 = x1; it.y1 = y1; it.n = count; it.a = alpha; it.w = s;
}

/* hit-testing data, filled every frame */
const hit = new Float32Array(MAXN * 5);
let hover = -1, hoverSeq = -1;

function build(){
  pc = 0;
  const hotHB = annHot === 'hb';
  const inDuplex = mode === 'duplex';
  sampleStrand(0); sampleStrand(1);
  pushRibbon(0, null); pushRibbon(1, null);
  if (!inDuplex) {
    sampleStrand(2); sampleStrand(3);
    const ok = (arr, s) => { const i0 = Math.floor(s / SUB), i1 = Math.min(n - 1, Math.ceil((s + 1) / SUB)); const a = Math.min(arr[i0], arr[i1]); return a < .97 ? 0 : (a - .97) / .03; };
    pushRibbon(2, s => ok(arrA, s)); pushRibbon(3, s => ok(arrB, s));
  }
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const th = TWIST * i + view.spin, a = seq[i], b = comp[i], g = grow[i];
    const sp = sep[i], offA = axisOff(0, sp), offB = axisOff(1, sp);
    const dim = hover >= 0 && hover !== i ? .55 : 1, hl = hover === i ? 1 : 0;
    baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offA, PUR[a], g, 0, 0, 0);
    const hA = pushHalf(a, g * dim, hl); const ax = HALF.x1, ay = HALF.y1, amx = HALF.mx, amy = HALF.my;
    hit[i * 5] = HALF.x0; hit[i * 5 + 1] = HALF.y0;
    baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offB, PUR[b], g, 0, 0, 0);
    const hB = pushHalf(b, g * dim, hl);
    hit[i * 5 + 2] = HALF.x0; hit[i * 5 + 3] = HALF.y0; hit[i * 5 + 4] = (hA.d + hB.d) / 2;
    if (sp < .1) pushBonds(ax, ay, HALF.x1, HALF.y1, a === 'A' || a === 'T' ? 2 : 3, (hA.d + hB.d) / 2 + .01, (1 - sp / .1) * g * dim, HALF.s);
    if (!inDuplex) {
      /* new partner on the top-strand template (sequence = comp) */
      const ea = easeOut(arrA[i]);
      if (arrA[i] > 0) {
        const m = 1 - ea, du = spawnA[i * 3] * m, dv = spawnA[i * 3 + 1] * m + Math.sin(arrA[i] * Math.PI) * 2, dw = spawnA[i * 3 + 2] * m;
        baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offA, PUR[b], 1, du, dv, dw);
        pushHalf(b, Math.min(1, arrA[i] * 2.5), 0);
        const bead = item(3, HALF.d + .02); bead.x0 = HALF.x0; bead.y0 = HALF.y0; bead.w = 1.5 * k * HALF.s; bead.c = paint(STR[2], 1, 0, HALF.d, Math.min(1, arrA[i] * 2.5));
        if (arrA[i] > .9) { baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offA, PUR[a], 1, 0, 0, 0); const tx = HALF.x1, ty = HALF.y1; baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offA, PUR[b], 1, 0, 0, 0); pushBonds(tx, ty, HALF.x1, HALF.y1, a === 'A' || a === 'T' ? 2 : 3, HALF.d + .01, (arrA[i] - .9) * 10, HALF.s); }
      }
      const eb = easeOut(arrB[i]);
      if (arrB[i] > 0) {
        const m = 1 - eb, du = spawnB[i * 3] * m, dv = spawnB[i * 3 + 1] * m - Math.sin(arrB[i] * Math.PI) * 2, dw = spawnB[i * 3 + 2] * m;
        baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offB, PUR[a], 1, du, dv, dw);
        pushHalf(a, Math.min(1, arrB[i] * 2.5), 0);
        const bead = item(3, HALF.d + .02); bead.x0 = HALF.x0; bead.y0 = HALF.y0; bead.w = 1.5 * k * HALF.s; bead.c = paint(STR[3], 1, 0, HALF.d, Math.min(1, arrB[i] * 2.5));
        if (arrB[i] > .9) { baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offB, PUR[b], 1, 0, 0, 0); const tx = HALF.x1, ty = HALF.y1; baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offB, PUR[a], 1, 0, 0, 0); pushBonds(tx, ty, HALF.x1, HALF.y1, a === 'A' || a === 'T' ? 2 : 3, HALF.d + .01, (arrB[i] - .9) * 10, HALF.s); }
      }
    }
  }
  /* direction chevrons on the old strands */
  for (let si = 0; si < 2; si++) {
    const buf = SB[si];
    for (let i = 2; i < n - 1; i += 5) {
      const s = i * SUB, o = s * 7, p = (s + 1) * 7;
      if (buf[o + 4] < RAD * .35) continue;
      let dx = buf[p + 5] - buf[o + 5], dy = buf[p + 6] - buf[o + 6];
      if (si === 1) { dx = -dx; dy = -dy; }
      const it = item(4, buf[o + 4] + .08); it.x0 = buf[o + 5]; it.y0 = buf[o + 6]; it.x1 = Math.atan2(dy, dx); it.w = k * .9 * FOCAL / (FOCAL - buf[o + 4]);
    }
  }
  list.length = pc;
  for (let i = 0; i < pc; i++) list[i] = pool[i];
  list.sort((p, q) => p.d - q.d);
  void hotHB;
}

function draw(){
  BG = bgNow();
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  /* axis */
  const u0 = (-(n - 1) / 2 - 1.5) * RISE, u1 = ((n - 1) / 2 + 1.5) * RISE;
  ctx.setLineDash([3, 6]); ctx.lineWidth = 1; ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? 'rgba(231,237,246,.18)' : 'rgba(15,27,45,.16)';
  if (mode === 'duplex') { ctx.beginPath(); proj(u0, 0, 0); ctx.moveTo(PX, PY); proj(u1, 0, 0); ctx.lineTo(PX, PY); ctx.stroke(); }
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  /* one-turn bracket */
  if (mode === 'duplex' && n >= 12) {
    const a0 = (-(n - 1) / 2) * RISE, a1 = a0 + 10.5 * RISE, vv = RAD + 7;
    ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? 'rgba(231,237,246,.42)' : 'rgba(15,27,45,.38)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    ctx.beginPath(); proj(a0, vv, 0); const bx0 = PX, by0 = PY; ctx.moveTo(PX, PY); proj(a1, vv, 0); ctx.lineTo(PX, PY); const bx1 = PX, by1 = PY; ctx.stroke();
    const nx = -(by1 - by0), ny = bx1 - bx0, nl = Math.hypot(nx, ny) || 1, tx = nx / nl * 5, ty = ny / nl * 5;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(bx0 - tx, by0 - ty); ctx.lineTo(bx0 + tx, by0 + ty); ctx.moveTo(bx1 - tx, by1 - ty); ctx.lineTo(bx1 + tx, by1 + ty); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.save(); ctx.translate((bx0 + bx1) / 2 + tx * 3.2, (by0 + by1) / 2 + ty * 3.2); ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(by1 - by0, bx1 - bx0));
    ctx.fillStyle = dark ? 'rgba(231,237,246,.7)' : 'rgba(15,27,45,.62)'; ctx.font = '600 11px ' + MONO; ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    ctx.fillText('one turn · 10.5 bp · ≈ 3.6 nm', 0, 0); ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.lineCap = 'round'; ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    const it = list[i];
    if (it.t === 0) {
      ctx.fillStyle = it.c; ctx.strokeStyle = it.c; ctx.lineWidth = .8;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(it.x0, it.y0); ctx.lineTo(it.x1, it.y1); ctx.lineTo(it.x2, it.y2); ctx.lineTo(it.x3, it.y3); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (it.t === 1) {
      if (it.hl) { ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? '#ffffff' : '#0f1b2d'; ctx.lineWidth = it.w + 4; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(it.x0, it.y0); ctx.lineTo(it.x1, it.y1); ctx.stroke(); }
      ctx.strokeStyle = it.c; ctx.lineWidth = it.w; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(it.x0, it.y0); ctx.lineTo(it.x1, it.y1); ctx.stroke();
      const dx = it.x1 - it.x0, dy = it.y1 - it.y0, l = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1, ox = -dy / l * it.w * .2, oy = dx / l * it.w * .2;
      ctx.strokeStyle = it.c2; ctx.lineWidth = it.w * .28; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(it.x0 + ox, it.y0 + oy - it.w * .1); ctx.lineTo(it.x1 + ox, it.y1 + oy - it.w * .1); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (it.t === 2) {
      if (it.a <= .01) continue;
      const dx = it.x1 - it.x0, dy = it.y1 - it.y0, l = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1, px = -dy / l, py = dx / l, gap = Math.max(2.2, it.w * k * .34);
      ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? 'rgba(231,237,246,' + (it.a * .9).toFixed(3) + ')' : 'rgba(15,27,45,' + (it.a * .75).toFixed(3) + ')';
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, k * .12 * it.w); ctx.setLineDash([Math.max(1.5, k * .18), Math.max(1.2, k * .12)]);
      ctx.beginPath();
      for (let b = 0; b < it.n; b++) { const o = (b - (it.n - 1) / 2) * gap; ctx.moveTo(it.x0 + px * o - dx * .12, it.y0 + py * o - dy * .12); ctx.lineTo(it.x1 + px * o + dx * .12, it.y1 + py * o + dy * .12); }
      ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
    } else if (it.t === 3) {
      ctx.fillStyle = it.c; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(it.x0, it.y0, Math.max(1.2, it.w * .5), 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
    } else if (it.t === 4) {
      const s = Math.max(3, it.w * .55);
      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(it.x0, it.y0); ctx.rotate(it.x1);
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.92)'; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1.4, s * .32);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-s * .5, -s * .6); ctx.lineTo(s * .35, 0); ctx.lineTo(-s * .5, s * .6); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
    }
  }
  /* strand ends */
  if (n > 1) {
    ctx.font = '700 ' + Math.round(Math.max(11, Math.min(15, k * 1.25))) + 'px ' + MONO;
    ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    const ends = [[0, 0, '5′'], [0, n - 1, '3′'], [1, 0, '3′'], [1, n - 1, '5′']];
    for (const [si, i, lab] of ends) {
      const th = TWIST * i + view.spin + angleOf(si), u = (i - (n - 1) / 2) * RISE + (i === 0 ? -4.8 : 4.8);
      proj(u, RAD * Math.cos(th) + axisOff(si, sep[i]), -RAD * Math.sin(th));
      ctx.fillStyle = si === 0 ? '#1aa698' : '#d0162d';
      if (dark) ctx.fillStyle = si === 0 ? '#5ce0d3' : '#ff5a6e';
      ctx.fillText(lab, PX, PY);
    }
  }
  /* pins */
  if (mode === 'duplex' && annAlpha > .02) drawPins();
}

/* =====================================================================
   Annotation targets + leader lines
   ===================================================================== */
const ANN = ['dir', 'bb', 'maj', 'min', 'hb'];
const annEls = [...document.querySelectorAll('.ann')];
let annHot = null, annAlpha = 1;
const targets = {};
function frontMost(lo, hi, fn){
  let best = null, bd = -1e9;
  const a = Math.max(0, Math.floor(n * lo)), b = Math.min(n - 1, Math.ceil(n * hi));
  for (let i = a; i <= b; i++) { const r = fn(i); if (r && r.d > bd) { bd = r.d; best = r; } }
  return best;
}
function surf(i, ang, rad){ const th = TWIST * i + view.spin + ang, u = (i - (n - 1) / 2) * RISE; proj(u, rad * Math.cos(th), -rad * Math.sin(th)); return { x: PX, y: PY, d: PD, i }; }
function computeTargets(){
  if (n < 4) { ANN.forEach(key => { targets[key] = null; }); return; }
  targets.dir = [frontMost(.8, 1, i => surf(i, 0, RAD))];
  targets.bb = [frontMost(.6, .8, i => surf(i, DELTA, RAD))];
  targets.maj = [frontMost(.42, .62, i => surf(i + .5, Math.PI + DELTA / 2, RAD * .96))];
  targets.min = [frontMost(.24, .44, i => surf(i + .5, DELTA / 2, RAD * .92))];
  const pairAt = want => frontMost(.02, .5, i => {
    const at = seq[i] === 'A' || seq[i] === 'T';
    if (want !== at) return null;
    const th = TWIST * i + view.spin, u = (i - (n - 1) / 2) * RISE, f = PUR[seq[i]] ? .545 : .455;
    const xv = RB * Math.cos(th), xw = -RB * Math.sin(th), pv = RB * Math.cos(th + DELTA), pw = -RB * Math.sin(th + DELTA);
    proj(u, xv + (pv - xv) * f, xw + (pw - xw) * f); return { x: PX, y: PY, d: PD, i };
  });
  targets.hb = [pairAt(true), pairAt(false)].filter(Boolean);
}
function drawPins(){
  ctx.font = '700 11px ' + SANS;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center'; ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
  ANN.forEach((key, idx) => {
    const ts = targets[key]; if (!ts) return;
    ts.forEach(t => {
      const hot = annHot === key, r = (hot ? 12 : 9.5) * (landscape ? 1 : .86);
      ctx.globalAlpha = annAlpha;
      ctx.fillStyle = hot ? '#1b6fd8' : (dark ? '#e7edf6' : '#0f1b2d');
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, r, 0, 6.2832); ctx.fill();
      ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? '#0b1220' : '#ffffff'; ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = hot ? '#fff' : (dark ? '#0b1220' : '#ffffff');
      ctx.fillText(String(idx + 1), t.x, t.y + .5);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    });
  });
}
const svgL = $('leaders');
const anchors = [];
function measureAnchors(){
  const panel = document.querySelector('.panel').getBoundingClientRect();
  anchors.length = 0;
  annEls.forEach(el => { const p = el.querySelector('.pin').getBoundingClientRect(); anchors.push({ x: p.left - 4, y: p.top + p.height / 2, edge: panel.left, top: panel.top, bottom: panel.bottom }); });
}
svgL.innerHTML = '<path d=""/>'.repeat(ANN.length * 2);
const leadPaths = ANN.map((key, i) => [svgL.children[i * 2], svgL.children[i * 2 + 1]]);
function updateLeaders(){
  if (getComputedStyle(svgL).display === 'none') return;
  const vr = viz.getBoundingClientRect();
  ANN.forEach((key, i) => {
    const a = anchors[i], ts = targets[key] || [];
    leadPaths[i].forEach((p, j) => {
      const t = ts[j];
      if (!a || !t || mode !== 'duplex' || a.y < a.top + 8 || a.y > a.bottom - 8) { p.setAttribute('d', ''); return; }
      const tx = vr.left + t.x, ty = vr.top + t.y, ex = a.edge - 18 - i * 3;
      const ang = Math.atan2(ty - a.y, tx - ex), r = annHot === key ? 12 : 9.5;
      p.setAttribute('d', 'M' + a.x.toFixed(1) + ' ' + a.y.toFixed(1) + 'H' + ex.toFixed(1) + 'L' + (tx - Math.cos(ang) * r).toFixed(1) + ' ' + (ty - Math.sin(ang) * r).toFixed(1));
      p.style.opacity = annAlpha.toFixed(2);
      p.setAttribute('class', annHot === key ? 'hot' : '');
    });
  });
}
annEls.forEach(el => {
  el.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => { annHot = el.dataset.k; el.classList.add('hot'); });
  el.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { annHot = null; el.classList.remove('hot'); });
});
document.querySelector('.panel').addEventListener('scroll', measureAnchors, { passive: true });

/* =====================================================================
   Sequence handling
   ===================================================================== */
const seqIn = $('seqIn'), seqMsg = $('seqMsg'), rowA = $('rowA'), rowB = $('rowB'), rowH = $('rowH'), stats = $('stats');
function setSequence(str, animate){
  const old = seq.join('');
  seq = str.split(''); comp = seq.map(c => PAIR[c]);
  const prevN = n; n = seq.length;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (animate && (i >= prevN || old[i] !== seq[i])) grow[i] = 0;
  cancelReplication();
  let a = '', b = '', h = '';
  seq.forEach((c, i) => {
    a += '<span class="b-' + c + '" data-i="' + i + '">' + c + '</span>';
    b += '<span class="b-' + comp[i] + '" data-i="' + i + '">' + comp[i] + '</span>';
    h += '<span data-i="' + i + '">' + (c === 'A' || c === 'T' ? '‖' : '⦀') + '</span>';
  });
  rowA.innerHTML = a; rowB.innerHTML = b; rowH.innerHTML = h;
  const gc = seq.filter(c => c === 'G' || c === 'C').length, at = n - gc;
  const tm = n < 14 ? 2 * at + 4 * gc : 64.9 + 41 * (gc - 16.4) / n;
  stats.innerHTML = '<span>Length <b>' + n + ' bp</b> (' + (n * .34).toFixed(1) + ' nm)</span><span>Turns <b>' + (n / 10.5).toFixed(2) + '</b></span><span>GC <b>' + Math.round(gc / n * 100) + '%</b></span><span>H-bonds <b>' + (2 * at + 3 * gc) + '</b></span><span>Tm ≈ <b>' + Math.round(tm) + ' °C</b> <span title="Basic estimate: Wallace rule under 14 bases, otherwise 64.9 + 41(GC − 16.4)/N">(basic estimate)</span></span>';
  frameGeometry(); computeTargets();
}
let msgT = 0;
seqIn.addEventListener('input', () => {
  const raw = seqIn.value, clean = raw.toUpperCase().replace(/[^ATGC]/g, '').slice(0, MAXN);
  if (clean !== raw) {
    const pos = seqIn.selectionStart - (raw.length - clean.length);
    seqIn.value = clean; try { seqIn.setSelectionRange(Math.max(0, pos), Math.max(0, pos)); } catch (e) {}
    if (/[^ATGCatgc]/.test(raw)) { seqMsg.textContent = 'Only A, T, G and C are DNA bases — other letters were left out.'; seqMsg.classList.add('warn'); clearTimeout(msgT); msgT = setTimeout(resetMsg, 2600); }
  }
  if (clean.length >= 2) setSequence(clean, true);
  else { seqMsg.textContent = 'Type at least two bases to build a helix.'; seqMsg.classList.add('warn'); }
});
function resetMsg(){ seqMsg.textContent = 'Type A, T, G or C (2 to 40 bases). The partner strand is generated for you.'; seqMsg.classList.remove('warn'); }
seqIn.addEventListener('blur', () => { if (seqIn.value.length < 2) { seqIn.value = seq.join(''); resetMsg(); } });
$('randBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const L = 18 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 13); let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < L; i++) s += 'ATGC'[Math.floor(Math.random() * 4)];
  seqIn.value = s; resetMsg(); setSequence(s, true);
});
function setSeqHighlight(i){
  if (i === hoverSeq) return;
  [rowA, rowB, rowH].forEach(r => { const o = r.children[hoverSeq]; if (o) o.classList.remove('hl'); const c = r.children[i]; if (c) c.classList.add('hl'); });
  hoverSeq = i;
}
[rowA, rowB, rowH].forEach(r => {
  r.addEventListener('pointerover', e => { const i = e.target.dataset && e.target.dataset.i; if (i !== undefined && mode === 'duplex') { hover = +i; setSeqHighlight(+i); } });
  r.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { hover = -1; setSeqHighlight(-1); });
});

/* =====================================================================
   Replication
   ===================================================================== */
const unzipBtn = $('unzipBtn'), unzipLbl = $('unzipLbl'), cap = $('cap');
function caption(t){ if (t) { cap.textContent = t; cap.classList.add('on'); } else cap.classList.remove('on'); }
function cancelReplication(){
  mode = 'duplex'; mt = 0; sep.fill(0); arrA.fill(0); arrB.fill(0);
  unzipLbl.textContent = 'Unzip & replicate'; unzipBtn.disabled = false; caption('');
}
function startReplication(){
  if (mode === 'duplex') {
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      spawnA[i * 3] = (Math.random() - .5) * 26; spawnA[i * 3 + 1] = 16 + Math.random() * 20; spawnA[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - .5) * 34;
      spawnB[i * 3] = (Math.random() - .5) * 26; spawnB[i * 3 + 1] = -(16 + Math.random() * 20); spawnB[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - .5) * 34;
      delA[i] = Math.random() * .45; delB[i] = Math.random() * .45;
    }
    mode = 'unzip'; mt = 0; hover = -1; setSeqHighlight(-1); $('tip').classList.remove('on');
    unzipBtn.disabled = true; unzipLbl.textContent = 'Replicating…';
    caption('Hydrogen bonds break and the strands separate');
  } else if (mode === 'done') {
    mode = 'rejoin'; mt = 0; unzipBtn.disabled = true; unzipLbl.textContent = 'Rejoining…';
    caption('Returning to the original double helix');
  }
}
unzipBtn.addEventListener('click', startReplication);
function stepReplication(dt){
  if (mode === 'duplex') return;
  const sp = reduced ? 2.2 : 1;
  mt += dt * sp;
  if (mode === 'unzip' || mode === 'synth' || mode === 'done') {
    const T1 = 2.6, fork = mt / T1 * 1.25;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      sep[i] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (fork - i / n) * 3.2));
      const start = .55 + (i / n) * T1 * .95;
      arrA[i] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (mt - start - delA[i]) / .95));
      arrB[i] = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (mt - start - delB[i]) / .95));
    }
    if (mode === 'unzip' && mt > 1.1) { mode = 'synth'; caption('Free nucleotides pair with each exposed base: A with T, G with C'); }
    if (mode === 'synth' && mt > .55 + T1 * .95 + .45 + .95 + .2) {
      mode = 'done'; unzipBtn.disabled = false; unzipLbl.textContent = 'Rejoin strands';
      caption('Two daughter helices, each keeping one original strand: semi-conservative replication');
    }
  } else if (mode === 'rejoin') {
    const k1 = Math.min(1, mt / 1.1);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { arrA[i] = Math.min(arrA[i], 1 - k1); arrB[i] = Math.min(arrB[i], 1 - k1); }
    if (mt > 1.1) {
      const f = (mt - 1.1) / 1.6 * 1.25;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) sep[i] = Math.min(sep[i], Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (1.25 - f - (1 - i / n)) * 3.2)));
    }
    if (mt > 2.9) { cancelReplication(); }
  }
}

/* =====================================================================
   Pointer, wheel, keyboard
   ===================================================================== */
const tip = $('tip');
const pts = new Map();
let dragging = false, lastX = 0, lastY = 0, velPerp = 0, pinch0 = 0, zoom0 = 1, idleAt = 0;
cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  pts.set(e.pointerId, [e.clientX, e.clientY]);
  try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
  if (pts.size === 1) { dragging = true; lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY; velPerp = 0; cv.classList.add('dragging'); }
  if (pts.size === 2) { const [a, b] = [...pts.values()]; pinch0 = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]); zoom0 = view.zoomT; }
});
cv.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (pts.has(e.pointerId)) pts.set(e.pointerId, [e.clientX, e.clientY]);
  if (pts.size === 2) { const [a, b] = [...pts.values()]; const d = Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1]); if (pinch0 > 0) view.zoomT = Math.max(.55, Math.min(2.8, zoom0 * d / pinch0)); return; }
  const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), mx = e.clientX - r.left, my = e.clientY - r.top;
  if (dragging) {
    const dx = e.clientX - lastX, dy = e.clientY - lastY; lastX = e.clientX; lastY = e.clientY;
    const perp = landscape ? dy * cR - dx * sR : dx, along = landscape ? dx * cR + dy * sR : -dy;
    view.spin += perp * .011; velPerp = perp * .011 * 60;
    view.tiltT = Math.max(-1.05, Math.min(1.05, view.tiltT + along * .005));
    idleAt = performance.now();
    return;
  }
  if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') pick(mx, my, e.clientX, e.clientY);
});
const endPtr = e => {
  pts.delete(e.pointerId);
  if (pts.size < 2) pinch0 = 0;
  if (pts.size === 0) { dragging = false; cv.classList.remove('dragging'); view.spinV = velPerp; }
  if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse' && e.type === 'pointerup') { const r = cv.getBoundingClientRect(); pick(e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top, e.clientX, e.clientY); }
};
cv.addEventListener('pointerup', endPtr);
cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', endPtr);
cv.addEventListener('pointerleave', e => { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && !dragging) { hover = -1; setSeqHighlight(-1); tip.classList.remove('on'); } });
cv.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const d = Math.max(-110, Math.min(110, e.deltaMode === 1 ? e.deltaY * 33 : e.deltaY));
  view.zoomT = Math.max(.55, Math.min(2.8, view.zoomT * Math.exp(-d * .0013)));
}, { passive: false });
function pick(mx, my, clientX, clientY){
  if (mode !== 'duplex') { hover = -1; tip.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  let best = -1, bd = 1e9;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const x0 = hit[i * 5], y0 = hit[i * 5 + 1], x1 = hit[i * 5 + 2], y1 = hit[i * 5 + 3];
    const dx = x1 - x0, dy = y1 - y0, L = dx * dx + dy * dy || 1;
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((mx - x0) * dx + (my - y0) * dy) / L));
    const d = Math.hypot(mx - (x0 + dx * t), my - (y0 + dy * t)) - hit[i * 5 + 4] * .25;
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = i; }
  }
  const lim = Math.max(10, k * 1.1);
  if (best >= 0 && bd < lim) {
    hover = best; setSeqHighlight(best);
    const a = seq[best], b = comp[best], at = a === 'A' || a === 'T';
    tip.innerHTML = '<b>' + a + (at ? '=' : '≡') + b + '</b> <i>· pair ' + (best + 1) + ' of ' + n + '</i><br>' + NAME[a] + ' (' + (PUR[a] ? 'purine' : 'pyrimidine') + ') with ' + NAME[b] + ' (' + (PUR[b] ? 'purine' : 'pyrimidine') + ')<br>' + (at ? 2 : 3) + ' hydrogen bonds';
    const vr = viz.getBoundingClientRect();
    let tx = clientX - vr.left, ty = clientY - vr.top;
    if (tx > vr.width - 270) tx -= 280;
    if (ty > vr.height - 110) ty -= 110;
    tip.style.left = tx + 'px'; tip.style.top = ty + 'px'; tip.classList.add('on');
  } else { hover = -1; setSeqHighlight(-1); tip.classList.remove('on'); }
}
cv.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const key = e.key; let used = true;
  if (key === 'ArrowLeft') view.spin -= .18; else if (key === 'ArrowRight') view.spin += .18;
  else if (key === 'ArrowUp') view.tiltT = Math.max(-1.05, view.tiltT - .12); else if (key === 'ArrowDown') view.tiltT = Math.min(1.05, view.tiltT + .12);
  else if (key === '+' || key === '=') view.zoomT = Math.min(2.8, view.zoomT * 1.2); else if (key === '-' || key === '_') view.zoomT = Math.max(.55, view.zoomT / 1.2);
  else if (key === ' ') toggleAuto(); else used = false;
  if (used) { e.preventDefault(); idleAt = performance.now(); }
});
const playBtn = $('playBtn');
function toggleAuto(){ view.auto = !view.auto; playBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', view.auto ? 'true' : 'false'); }
playBtn.addEventListener('click', toggleAuto);
$('zoomIn').addEventListener('click', () => { view.zoomT = Math.min(2.8, view.zoomT * 1.25); });
$('zoomOut').addEventListener('click', () => { view.zoomT = Math.max(.55, view.zoomT / 1.25); });
$('resetBtn').addEventListener('click', () => { view.zoomT = 1; view.tiltT = .16; view.spinV = 0; });

/* =====================================================================
   Loop
   ===================================================================== */
let last = 0, raf = 0, leaderTick = 0;
function frame(now){
  const dt = last ? Math.min(.05, (now - last) / 1000) : .016; last = now;
  const autoSpeed = view.auto ? (reduced ? .12 : .42) : 0;
  if (!dragging) {
    view.spinV += (autoSpeed - view.spinV) * Math.min(1, dt * 1.6);
    view.spin += view.spinV * dt;
  }
  view.tilt += (view.tiltT - view.tilt) * Math.min(1, dt * 6);
  view.zoom += (view.zoomT - view.zoom) * Math.min(1, dt * 7);
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) if (grow[i] < 1) grow[i] = Math.min(1, grow[i] + dt * (reduced ? 6 : 2.4));
  stepReplication(dt);
  annAlpha += ((mode === 'duplex' ? 1 : 0) - annAlpha) * Math.min(1, dt * 6);
  frameGeometry();
  build();
  computeTargets();
  draw();
  leaderTick += dt;
  if (leaderTick > .5) { leaderTick = 0; measureAnchors(); }
  updateLeaders();
  raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
function start(){ if (!raf) { last = 0; raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame); } }
function stop(){ if (raf) cancelAnimationFrame(raf); raf = 0; }
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { document.hidden ? stop() : start(); });
let rt = 0;
window.addEventListener('resize', () => { clearTimeout(rt); rt = setTimeout(layout, 60); });

layout();
setSequence(seqIn.value.toUpperCase().replace(/[^ATGC]/g, '') || 'ATGGCTAGCCTAAGTTCGGATCAC', false);
start();
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>


================================================================
FILE: apps/dna/app.nim
================================================================
## 052 Helix — a port of 052-dna-helix.html to Yantra.
##
## THE SOURCE IS A HAND-WRITTEN 3D PIPELINE ON A 2D CANVAS, NOT A PAGE OF HTML.
## `proj()` (html:277) spins a model point about the helix axis, applies a tilt,
## does a perspective divide against FOCAL and then an in-plane roll. The item
## pool + `list.sort((p,q)=>p.d-q.d)` is a painter's algorithm over ~2000
## ribbons/rungs/beads. Yantra has no perspective canvas transform, so the same
## maths runs here and every primitive is issued as a `Draw` verb.
##
## THE WHOLE PAGE IS ONE `canvas` AT 1280x816, not a node tree: the source is a
## CSS grid (1fr / 420px) whose two columns are a canvas and an annotation
## panel, and every string in the panel is typeset at a computed position rather
## than laid out. That keeps the port faithful to the source's own pipeline at
## the cost of losing automatic wrapping, so `wrapText` does the job the CSS
## `text-wrap` does and the estimate is documented at that proc.
##
## THE DOTTED "LAB BENCH" BACKDROP IS BAKED ONCE INTO A `Layer`.
## `radial-gradient(rgba(15,27,45,.075) 1.1px, transparent 1.3px)` at
## `background-size 22px 22px` over a 860x756 area is ~1,326 antialiased dots a
## frame; re-rasterising that every frame is the single most expensive thing in
## the scene and it never changes. It is written once as raw pixels (html:45) and
## blitted with `Draw.layer` — the same trick `apps/gallery` uses for its
## corridor and sprite layers.

import yantra
import std/[math, algorithm]

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── stage geometry
## The reference frame is 1280x816 (`.board/frames/ref052.png`). At 1280 wide
## neither media query fires (`max-width:1180` needs a narrower window), so the
## grid is exactly `minmax(0,1fr) 420px` (html:44).
const
  SW = 1280.0'f32          ## innerWidth
  SH = 816.0'f32           ## innerHeight
  TOP_H = 60.0'f32        ## .top height
  PANEL_W = 420.0'f32     ## .app grid column 2
  VIZ_X = 0.0'f32
  VIZ_Y = TOP_H
  VW = SW - PANEL_W        ## 860
  VH = SH - TOP_H          ## 756

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── the palette
## html:10-16, the light theme only. The dark-mode toggle is a control, not a
## layout change, so the port ships the default theme (html:242 `dark = false`).
##
## EVERY constant here carries an explicit 0xFF alpha byte. `Draw` takes
## `argb: uint32` in AARRGGBB, so a CSS hex pasted straight in (`0xF7F9FB`) has
## alpha 0x00 and draws **fully transparent** — it compiles, it runs, it crashes
## nothing, and it is invisible. That cost several rounds here: the page
## background silently vanished and the host's window clear colour (16,11,11) came
## through instead. Use the `rgba` helper at :86 for anything translucent.
var
  C_BG     = 0xFFF7F9FBu32  ## --bg (theme-swapped by applyTheme)
  C_PANEL  = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32 ## --panel
  C_PANEL2 = 0xFFF2F5F8'u32  ## --panel2
  C_INK    = 0xFF0F1B2Du32   ## --ink
  C_INK2   = 0xFF46566Bu32   ## --ink2
  C_INK3   = 0xFF8793A4'u32  ## --ink3
  C_LINE   = 0xFFE2E7EE'u32  ## --line
  C_FOCUS  = 0xFF1B6FD8'u32  ## --focus
  C_TIP    = 0xFF0F1B2Du32   ## --tip
  C_TIPINK = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32  ## --tip-ink
  ## the seq-strip colours differ from the 3D BASE table (html:105) — they are
  ## darkened for text legibility on --panel2 (html:106 swaps them in the dark).
  C_bA = 0xFFE08500'u32
  C_bT = 0xFFD9962Fu32
  ## Renamed from C_bG: Nim identifiers are case-insensitive AND ignore underscores,
  ## so this collided with C_BG (the --bg page colour at :46) and the file would not
  ## compile. The G nucleotide's base, not the background.
  C_bGrn = 0xFF6A4C93'u32
  C_bC = 0xFF5F9A0E'u32
const
  C_TEAL   = 0xFF2EC4B6'u32
  C_RED    = 0xFFE71D36'u32
  C_A      = 0xFFFF9F1C'u32
  C_T      = 0xFFFFBF69'u32
  C_G      = 0xFF6A4C93'u32
  C_C      = 0xFF8AC926'u32

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── numeric helpers
proc rnd32(x: float32): int32 =
  ## round() — `std/math`'s float32 `round` becomes a wasm IMPORT the host does
  ## not provide, which refuses to instantiate the whole module
  ## (skills/yantra/reference/23-wasm-import-gate.md). `apps/gallery/app.nim:87`
  ## carries the same helper and the same reason.
  int32(if x < 0: x - 0.5 else: x + 0.5)

proc cl(f, lo, hi: float32): float32 =
  if f < lo: lo elif f > hi: hi else: f

proc rgba(r, g, b: int32; a: float32): uint32 =
  proc q(v: int32): int32 = (if v < 0: 0 elif v > 255: 255 else: v)
  result = (uint32(q(rnd32(a * 255.0'f32))) shl 24) or
           (uint32(q(r)) shl 16) or (uint32(q(g)) shl 8) or uint32(q(b))

proc withA(c: uint32; a: float32): uint32 =
  (c and 0x00FFFFFF'u32) or
    (uint32(max(0'i32, min(255'i32, rnd32(a * 255.0'f32)))) shl 24)

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────── model constants (Å)
## html:255-261, verbatim.
const
  MAXN = 40
  SUB = 4
  TWIST = 2.0'f32 * PI / 10.5'f32      ## 360deg / 10.5 bp
  RISE = 3.4'f32                      ## 3.4 A per base pair
  RAD  = 10.0'f32                     ## 10 A helix radius
  DELTA = 135.0'f32 * PI / 180.0'f32   ## backbone offset around the axis
  FOCAL = 95.0'f32
  HW = 1.55'f32                       ## half the ribbon width
  RB = 8.4'f32                        ## base-pair radius

const PAIR = ['T', 'A', 'C', 'G']      ## indexed by A,T,G,C -> complement
const BASE_RGB: array[4, array[3, int32]] = [[255, 159, 28], [255, 191, 105],
                                             [106, 76, 147], [138, 201, 38]]
const BASE_COL: array[4, uint32] = [C_A, C_T, C_G, C_C]
const PUR: array[4, bool] = [true, false, true, false]   ## A,T,G,C are purine
const STR_RGB: array[4, array[3, int32]] = [[46, 196, 182], [231, 29, 54],
                                             [244, 140, 154], [138, 226, 216]]

proc baseIdx(c: char): int32 =
  case c
  of 'A': 0
  of 'T': 1
  of 'G': 2
  of 'C': 3
  else: 0

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── app state
var seqStr = "ATGGCTAGCCTAAGTTCGGATCAC"   ## html:204, the input's default
var compStr = ""
var grow: array[MAXN, float32]
var sepA: array[MAXN, float32]

var spin = 0.6'f32        ## view.spin
var tilt = 0.16'f32       ## view.tilt (eased toward tiltT)
var tiltT = 0.16'f32
var zoom = 1.0'f32
var zoomT = 1.0'f32
var spinV = 0.0'f32
var autoRot = true
var hover = -1
var dragging = false
var lastPX, lastPY = 0.0'f32

# ─────────────────────────────── interaction state (driven by play.nim)
## Declared here — ahead of build()/drawPinsOv()/drawLeadersOv()/bakeDotField(),
## which all read them — and mutated by the procs in apps/dna/play.nim.
var mode = 0               ## html:267/651: 0 duplex 1 unzip 2 synth 3 done 4 rejoin
var mt = 0.0'f32           ## replication clock
var hovSeq = -1            ## seq-strip highlight index (html:632)
var annHot = -1            ## hovered annotation row 0..4 (html:586)
var annAlpha = 1.0'f32     ## pin/leader opacity, fades out while replicating
var gScroll = 0.0'f32      ## .panel scrollTop (html:69 overflow-y:auto)
var darkTheme = false      ## html:242
var hit: array[MAXN * 5, float32]     ## rung pick segments, html:409-412
var spawnA: array[MAXN * 3, float32]  ## html:266 arrival origins
var spawnB: array[MAXN * 3, float32]
var delA: array[MAXN, float32]        ## html:266 arrival delays
var delB: array[MAXN, float32]

proc easeOut(x: float32): float32 =
  ## html:329. Used by build(); must precede it.
  1.0'f32 - (1.0'f32 - x) * (1.0'f32 - x) * (1.0'f32 - x)

proc n(): int32 = int32(seqStr.len)
proc compAt(i: int32): char = compStr[i]

proc rebuild() =
  compStr = newStringOfCap(seqStr.len)
  for c in seqStr:
    compStr.add PAIR[baseIdx(c)]
  ## Nim zeroes every global, so `grow` starts at 0 and `sepA`/`arrA`/`arrB` are
  ## correct at 0 — but html:264 is `new Float32Array(MAXN).fill(1)`. Without this
  ## the growth pulse never fires and every base pair is drawn at alpha 0.
  for i in 0 ..< MAXN:
    grow[i] = 1.0'f32

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── projection (html:277)
var cT = 1.0'f32
var sT = 0.0'f32
var cR = 1.0'f32
var sR = 0.0'f32
var cx0 = 0.0'f32
var cy0 = 0.0'f32
var kk = 0.0'f32
var PX = 0.0'f32
var PY = 0.0'f32
var PD = 0.0'f32
var PS = 0.0'f32

proc proj(u, v, w: float32) =
  let u2 = u * cT - w * sT
  let w2 = u * sT + w * cT
  let s = FOCAL / (FOCAL - w2)
  let x = u2 * s * kk
  let y = v * s * kk
  PX = cx0 + x * cR - y * sR
  PY = cy0 + x * sR + y * cR
  PD = w2
  PS = s

proc frameGeometry() =
  ## html:290-297, with the `landscape` test already decided by the fixed size:
  ## 860 > 756*1.05 = 793.8, so the diagonal roll of -0.24 rad applies.
  let landscape = true
  let roll = -0.24'f32
  cR = cos(roll)
  sR = sin(roll)
  cT = cos(tilt)
  sT = sin(tilt)
  let L = VW * 0.8'f32
  let risePx = min(46.0'f32, L / max(float32(n()), 8.0'f32))
  kk = risePx / RISE * zoom
  cx0 = VW * 0.47'f32
  cy0 = VH * 0.56'f32
  discard landscape

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── shading (html:308)
var BG: array[3, int32] = [247, 249, 251]

const LU = -0.28'f32
const LV = -0.5'f32
const LW = 0.82'f32

proc lightOf(th: float32): (float32, float32) =
  let nv = cos(th)
  let nw0 = -sin(th)
  let nu = -nw0 * sT
  let nw = nw0 * cT
  let dd = nu * LU + nv * LV + nw * LW
  let lit = 0.42'f32 + 0.58'f32 * max(0.0'f32, dd) + (if nw < 0: -0.08'f32 else: 0.0'f32)
  let spec = pow(max(0.0'f32, dd), 22.0'f32) * 0.65'f32
  (lit, spec)

proc paint(rgb: array[3, int32]; lit, spec, depth, alpha: float32): uint32 =
  ## html:302-306 — depth fade is theme-dependent (html:303: dark 0.66, light 0.60)
  let fade = pow(cl((RAD - depth) / (2.0'f32 * RAD), 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32), 1.25'f32) *
    (if darkTheme: 0.66'f32 else: 0.60'f32)
  var r = rgb[0].float32 * lit + 255.0'f32 * spec
  var g = rgb[1].float32 * lit + 255.0'f32 * spec
  var b = rgb[2].float32 * lit + 255.0'f32 * spec
  r = min(255.0'f32, r + (BG[0].float32 - r) * fade)
  g = min(255.0'f32, g + (BG[1].float32 - g) * fade)
  b = min(255.0'f32, b + (BG[2].float32 - b) * fade)
  rgba(rnd32(r), rnd32(g), rnd32(b), alpha)

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── item pool
## html:319-326. One flat array, sorted back-to-front, is the whole depth model.
type Item = object
  t: int32
  dp: float32
  x0, y0, x1, y1, x2, y2, x3, y3: float32
  w: float32
  c, c2: uint32
  n: int32
  a: float32
  hl: int32            ## html:380/477 hover outline flag (0/1)

const MAXIT = 6000
var pool: array[MAXIT, Item]
var pc = 0

proc item(t: int32; dd: float32): int32 =
  if pc >= MAXIT: return -1
  pool[pc] = Item(t: t, dp: dd, c: 0'u32, c2: 0'u32, a: 1.0'f32, hl: 0)
  inc pc
  pc - 1

proc angleOf(which: int32): float32 =
  if which == 1 or which == 2: DELTA else: 0.0'f32

proc smoothF(x: float32): float32 =
  if x <= 0: 0.0'f32 elif x >= 1: 1.0'f32 else: x * x * (3.0'f32 - 2.0'f32 * x)

const DSEP = RAD * 1.32'f32

proc axisOff(which: int32; s: float32): float32 =
  (if which == 0 or which == 2: 1.0'f32 else: -1.0'f32) * DSEP * smoothF(s)

# strand sample buffers: xL,yL,xR,yR,depth,x,y per sample
const MAXM = (MAXN - 1) * SUB + 1
var SB: array[4, array[MAXM, array[7, float32]]]

proc sampleStrand(si: int32) =
  let nn = n()
  let m = (nn - 1) * SUB + 1
  var s = 0
  while s < m:
    let t = float32(s) / float32(SUB)
    let i0 = int32(floor(t))
    let i1 = min(nn - 1, i0 + 1)
    let fr = t - float32(i0)
    let sp = sepA[i0] + (sepA[i1] - sepA[i0]) * fr
    let off = axisOff(si, sp)
    let th = TWIST * t + spin + angleOf(si)
    let u = (t - float32(nn - 1) / 2.0'f32) * RISE
    let v = RAD * cos(th) + off
    let w = -RAD * sin(th)
    proj(u - HW, v, w); SB[si][s][0] = PX; SB[si][s][1] = PY; let d0 = PD
    proj(u + HW, v, w); SB[si][s][2] = PX; SB[si][s][3] = PY
    proj(u, v, w);      SB[si][s][4] = (d0 + PD) / 2.0'f32; SB[si][s][5] = PX; SB[si][s][6] = PY
    inc s

proc arrAOrB(s, which: int32): float32
## Forward declaration. arrAOrB is DEFINED at :302 and called from pushRibbon at :277,
## which Nim rejects outright - "undeclared identifier: 'arrAOrB'" - because a proc must
## be declared before use. Per AGENTS.md the fix is a forward declaration, not
## reordering the file.

proc pushRibbon(si: int32; alphaAt: int32) =
  ## alphaAt: -1 = fully opaque (the two original strands), otherwise the index
  ## into `arrA`/`arrB`, which the unzip phase ramps in.
  let nn = n()
  let m = (nn - 1) * SUB + 1
  var s = 0
  while s < m - 1:
    var a = 1.0'f32
    if alphaAt >= 0:
      a = arrAOrB(s, alphaAt)
    if a > 0.01'f32:
      let dd = (SB[si][s][4] + SB[si][s + 1][4]) / 2.0'f32
      let it = item(0, dd)
      if it >= 0:
        pool[it].x0 = SB[si][s][0]; pool[it].y0 = SB[si][s][1]
        pool[it].x1 = SB[si][s][2]; pool[it].y1 = SB[si][s][3]
        pool[it].x2 = SB[si][s + 1][2]; pool[it].y2 = SB[si][s + 1][3]
        pool[it].x3 = SB[si][s + 1][0]; pool[it].y3 = SB[si][s + 1][1]
        let tt = (float32(s) + 0.5'f32) / float32(SUB)
        let th = TWIST * tt + spin + angleOf(si)
        let L = lightOf(th)
        pool[it].c = paint(STR_RGB[si], L[0], L[1], dd, a)
      ## the phosphate bead at every SUB-th sample (html:357)
      if s mod SUB == 0 and s > 0 and s < m - SUB:
        let bd = item(3, SB[si][s][4] + 0.05'f32)
        if bd >= 0:
          pool[bd].x0 = SB[si][s][5]
          pool[bd].y0 = SB[si][s][6]
          pool[bd].w = 1.05'f32 * kk * FOCAL / (FOCAL - SB[si][s][4])
          ## [255, 255, 255] inferred as array[0..2, int]; paint wants array[3, int32].
          pool[bd].c = paint([255'i32, 255'i32, 255'i32], 0.95'f32, 0.0'f32,
                             SB[si][s][4], a * 0.55'f32)
    inc s

var arrA, arrB: array[MAXN, float32]

proc arrAOrB(s, which: int32): float32 =
  ## html:400 — a new strand only draws where the fork has already passed.
  let i0 = int32(floor(float32(s) / float32(SUB)))
  let i1 = min(n() - 1, int32(ceil((float32(s) + 1.0'f32) / float32(SUB))))
  let a = (if which == 2: arrA[i0] else: arrB[i0])
  let b = (if which == 2: arrA[i1] else: arrB[i1])
  let m = min(a, b)
  if m < 0.97'f32: 0.0'f32 else: (m - 0.97'f32) / 0.03'f32

# one base half, html:363
type Half = object
  x0, y0, x1, y1: float32
  d, s: float32
  mx, my: float32

proc baseHalf(i: int32; thX, thP, off: float32; purine: bool;
              g, du, dv, dw: float32): Half =
  let nn = n()
  let u = (float32(i) - float32(nn - 1) / 2.0'f32) * RISE + du
  let xv = RB * cos(thX) + off + dv
  let xw = -RB * sin(thX) + dw
  let pv = RB * cos(thP) + off + dv
  let pw = -RB * sin(thP) + dw
  let f = (if purine: 0.545'f32 else: 0.455'f32) - 0.032'f32
  let ev = xv + (pv - xv) * f * g
  let ew = xw + (pw - xw) * f * g
  proj(u, xv, xw); result.x0 = PX; result.y0 = PY; let d0 = PD
  proj(u, ev, ew); result.x1 = PX; result.y1 = PY
  result.d = (d0 + PD) / 2.0'f32; result.s = PS
  let mf = (if purine: 0.545'f32 else: 0.455'f32)
  proj(u, xv + (pv - xv) * mf, xw + (pw - xw) * mf)
  result.mx = PX; result.my = PY

proc pushHalf(h: Half; ch: char; alpha: float32; hl = 0) =
  let it = item(1, h.d)
  if it < 0: return
  pool[it].x0 = h.x0; pool[it].y0 = h.y0; pool[it].x1 = h.x1; pool[it].y1 = h.y1
  pool[it].w = 1.25'f32 * kk * h.s
  pool[it].c = paint(BASE_RGB[baseIdx(ch)], 1.0'f32, 0.0'f32, h.d, alpha)
  pool[it].c2 = paint([255'i32, 255'i32, 255'i32], 1.0'f32, 0.0'f32, h.d,
                        alpha * 0.38'f32)
  pool[it].hl = hl

proc pushBonds(x0, y0, x1, y1: float32; count: int32; d, alpha, s: float32) =
  let it = item(2, d)
  if it < 0: return
  pool[it].x0 = x0; pool[it].y0 = y0; pool[it].x1 = x1; pool[it].y1 = y1
  pool[it].n = count; pool[it].a = alpha; pool[it].w = s

var order: seq[int32]

proc build() =
  pc = 0
  sampleStrand(0); sampleStrand(1)
  pushRibbon(0, -1); pushRibbon(1, -1)
  if mode != 0:
    ## html:398-402 — the two daughter backbones, gated per-sample by arrA/arrB.
    sampleStrand(2); sampleStrand(3)
    pushRibbon(2, 2); pushRibbon(3, 3)
  let nn = n()
  var i = 0
  while i < nn:
    let th = TWIST * float32(i) + spin
    let a = seqStr[i]
    let b = compAt(i)
    let g = grow[i]
    let sp = sepA[i]
    let offA = axisOff(0, sp)
    let offB = axisOff(1, sp)
    let dim = (if hover >= 0 and hover != i: 0.55'f32 else: 1.0'f32)
    let hl = (if hover == i: 1 else: 0)

    let hA0 = baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offA, PUR[baseIdx(a)], g, 0, 0, 0)
    pushHalf(hA0, a, g * dim, hl)
    let hA = baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offA, PUR[baseIdx(a)], g, 0, 0, 0)
    let hB0 = baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offB, PUR[baseIdx(b)], g, 0, 0, 0)
    pushHalf(hB0, b, g * dim, hl)
    let hB = baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offB, PUR[baseIdx(b)], g, 0, 0, 0)
    hit[i * 5] = hA.x0; hit[i * 5 + 1] = hA.y0
    hit[i * 5 + 2] = hB.x0; hit[i * 5 + 3] = hB.y0
    hit[i * 5 + 4] = (hA.d + hB.d) / 2.0'f32
    let cnt = (if a == 'A' or a == 'T': 2 else: 3)
    if sp < 0.1'f32:
      pushBonds(hA.x1, hA.y1, hB.x1, hB.y1, cnt, (hA.d + hB.d) / 2.0'f32 + 0.01'f32,
                (1.0'f32 - sp / 0.1'f32) * g * dim, hB.s)
    if mode != 0:
      ## html:414-432 — arriving nucleotides on each exposed template.
      let ea = easeOut(arrA[i])
      if arrA[i] > 0:
        let mA = 1.0'f32 - ea
        let hN = baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offA, PUR[baseIdx(b)], 1.0'f32,
                          spawnA[i * 3] * mA,
                          spawnA[i * 3 + 1] * mA + sin(arrA[i] * PI) * 2.0'f32,
                          spawnA[i * 3 + 2] * mA)
        pushHalf(hN, b, min(1.0'f32, arrA[i] * 2.5'f32))
        let bdA = item(3, hN.d + 0.02'f32)
        if bdA >= 0:
          pool[bdA].x0 = hN.x0; pool[bdA].y0 = hN.y0
          pool[bdA].w = 1.5'f32 * kk * hN.s
          pool[bdA].c = paint(STR_RGB[2], 1.0'f32, 0.0'f32, hN.d,
                              min(1.0'f32, arrA[i] * 2.5'f32))
        if arrA[i] > 0.9'f32:
          let t1 = baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offA, PUR[baseIdx(a)],
                            1.0'f32, 0, 0, 0)
          let t2 = baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offA, PUR[baseIdx(b)],
                            1.0'f32, 0, 0, 0)
          pushBonds(t1.x1, t1.y1, t2.x1, t2.y1, cnt, t2.d + 0.01'f32,
                    (arrA[i] - 0.9'f32) * 10.0'f32, t2.s)
      let eb = easeOut(arrB[i])
      if arrB[i] > 0:
        let mB = 1.0'f32 - eb
        let hM = baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offB, PUR[baseIdx(a)], 1.0'f32,
                          spawnB[i * 3] * mB,
                          spawnB[i * 3 + 1] * mB - sin(arrB[i] * PI) * 2.0'f32,
                          spawnB[i * 3 + 2] * mB)
        pushHalf(hM, a, min(1.0'f32, arrB[i] * 2.5'f32))
        let bdB = item(3, hM.d + 0.02'f32)
        if bdB >= 0:
          pool[bdB].x0 = hM.x0; pool[bdB].y0 = hM.y0
          pool[bdB].w = 1.5'f32 * kk * hM.s
          pool[bdB].c = paint(STR_RGB[3], 1.0'f32, 0.0'f32, hM.d,
                              min(1.0'f32, arrB[i] * 2.5'f32))
        if arrB[i] > 0.9'f32:
          let u1 = baseHalf(i, th + DELTA, th, offB, PUR[baseIdx(b)],
                            1.0'f32, 0, 0, 0)
          let u2 = baseHalf(i, th, th + DELTA, offB, PUR[baseIdx(a)],
                            1.0'f32, 0, 0, 0)
          pushBonds(u1.x1, u1.y1, u2.x1, u2.y1, cnt, u2.d + 0.01'f32,
                    (arrB[i] - 0.9'f32) * 10.0'f32, u2.s)
    inc i
  ## 5'->3' direction chevrons on the two original strands (html:435)
  var si = 0
  while si < 2:
    var i = 2
    while i < nn - 1:
      let s = i * SUB
      if SB[si][s][4] >= RAD * 0.35'f32:
        var dx = SB[si][s + 1][5] - SB[si][s][5]
        var dy = SB[si][s + 1][6] - SB[si][s][6]
        if si == 1:
          dx = -dx; dy = -dy
        let it = item(4, SB[si][s][4] + 0.08'f32)
        if it >= 0:
          pool[it].x0 = SB[si][s][5]
          pool[it].y0 = SB[si][s][6]
          pool[it].x1 = arctan2(dy, dx) * 180.0'f32 / PI
          pool[it].w = kk * 0.9'f32 * FOCAL / (FOCAL - SB[si][s][4])
      i += 5
    inc si
  order = newSeq[int32](pc)
  var j = 0
  while j < pc:
    order[j] = j
    inc j
  ## The comparator returns `int`, not `int32`: Nim's `sort` from std/algorithm
  ## expects proc(x, y: T): int, and an int32 return is a type mismatch.
  order.sort(proc (a, b: int32): int =
    if pool[a].dp < pool[b].dp: -1 elif pool[a].dp > pool[b].dp: 1 else: 0)

proc paintPool(d: var Draw) =
  ## The missing half of this port: turn the sorted item pool into Draw commands.
  ## `frameGeometry` produces `order` (painter's order, far to near) and each
  ## `pool[it]` carries a type tag, a depth, a colour and the vertex data its type
  ## needs. Everything up to here was 3D maths with nowhere to go.
  for k in 0 ..< pc:
    let i = order[k]
    let it = pool[i]
    if false:
      ## V2 FIX: html has NO depth cull (only `it.a <= .01`, html:482, for t==2
      ## only). dp = w2 is NEGATIVE on the far half of the helix, so this guard
      ## silently dropped the entire back of the molecule.
      discard
    if it.t == 0:
      ## V3 FIX: t==0 is the STRAND RIBBON (html:473-475) - a quad (x0,y0)
      ## (x1,y1) (x2,y2) (x3,y3), filled AND stroked at 0.8 in the same colour.
      ## paintPool had NO t==0 branch at all, so both backbones were dropped.
      d = d.moveTo(it.x0, it.y0)
      d = d.lineTo(it.x1, it.y1)
      d = d.lineTo(it.x2, it.y2)
      d = d.lineTo(it.x3, it.y3)
      d = d.close
      d = d.fill(it.c)
      d = d.stroke(it.c, 0.8'f32)
    elif it.t == 1:
      ## V1 FIX: t==1 is a BASE HALF (html:476-480), not a ribbon quad. It is a
      ## thick round-capped LINE of width w from (x0,y0) to (x1,y1), plus a thin
      ## white highlight offset along the normal. The port drew it as a 4-vertex
      ## quad whose 3rd/4th vertices are never written by pushHalf, i.e. (0,0).
      ## Gemini 2.3: hover outline (html:477) — (w+4) stroke behind the hovered rung.
      let hw = it.w
      if it.hl != 0:
        let hc = if darkTheme: 0xFFFFFFFF'u32 else: 0xFF0F1B2D'u32
        d = d.line(it.x0, it.y0, it.x1, it.y1, hc, hw + 4.0'f32)
      d = d.line(it.x0, it.y0, it.x1, it.y1, it.c, hw)
      let dx = it.x1 - it.x0
      let dy = it.y1 - it.y0
      let l = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
      if l > 0.0'f32:
        let ox = -dy / l * hw * 0.2'f32
        let oy = dx / l * hw * 0.2'f32
        d = d.line(it.x0 + ox, it.y0 + oy - hw * 0.1'f32,
                   it.x1 + ox, it.y1 + oy - hw * 0.1'f32, it.c2, hw * 0.28'f32)
    elif it.t == 2:
      ## V5 FIX: t==2 is the H-BOND rung (html:481-488) - `count` (2 or 3) short
      ## strokes spread across the rung, not a disc. The port drew a circle of
      ## radius it.w at (x0,y0) with it.c still 0 (pushBonds never paints a
      ## colour), so every bond was a transparent blob and nothing was drawn.
      ## sdk/ui/paint.nim has no setLineDash (see FINDINGS.md, engine note E11),
      ## so the dashes are drawn solid here - the closest available.
      if it.a > 0.01'f32:
        let bdx = it.x1 - it.x0
        let bdy = it.y1 - it.y0
        let bl = sqrt(bdx * bdx + bdy * bdy)
        if bl > 0.0'f32:
          let ppx = -bdy / bl
          let ppy = bdx / bl
          let gap = max(2.2'f32, it.w * kk * 0.34'f32)
          let lw = max(1.0'f32, kk * 0.12'f32 * it.w)
          for b in 0 ..< it.n:
            let o = (float32(b) - float32(it.n - 1) / 2.0'f32) * gap
            d = d.line(it.x0 + ppx * o - bdx * 0.12'f32, it.y0 + ppy * o - bdy * 0.12'f32,
                       it.x1 + ppx * o + bdx * 0.12'f32, it.y1 + ppy * o + bdy * 0.12'f32,
                       withA(C_INK, it.a * (if darkTheme: 0.90'f32 else: 0.75'f32)), lw)
    elif it.t == 3:
      ## V5 FIX: phosphate bead (html:489-490) - `ctx.arc(x, y, Math.max(1.2, w*.5))`.
      ## The port passed `w` straight through as the radius, i.e. 2x, i.e. 4x area.
      d = d.circle(it.x0, it.y0, max(1.2'f32, it.w * 0.5'f32), it.c)
    elif it.t == 4:
      ## V5 FIX: 5'->3' chevron (html:491-496) - a stroked arrowhead of half-size
      ## s = max(3, w*.55), rotated to x1 degrees. The port drew a single 1px line
      ## of length w, which is a different shape entirely.
      let s = max(3.0'f32, it.w * 0.55'f32)
      let a = it.x1 * PI / 180.0'f32
      let ca = cos(a)
      let sa = sin(a)
      proc rot(dx, dy: float32): (float32, float32) =
        (dx * ca - dy * sa, dx * sa + dy * ca)
      let p1 = rot(-s * 0.5'f32, -s * 0.6'f32)
      let p2 = rot(s * 0.35'f32, 0.0'f32)
      let p3 = rot(-s * 0.5'f32, s * 0.6'f32)
      let lw = max(1.4'f32, s * 0.32'f32)
      let cc = rgba(255'i32, 255'i32, 255'i32, 0.92'f32)
      d = d.line(it.x0 + p1[0], it.y0 + p1[1], it.x0 + p2[0], it.y0 + p2[1], cc, lw)
      d = d.line(it.x0 + p2[0], it.y0 + p2[1], it.x0 + p3[0], it.y0 + p3[1], cc, lw)

var ready = false
var viz: Node
var page0: Node
var dotField: Layer
var dotBaked = false

proc bakeDotField() =
  ## html:45 — `.viz` is painted with
  ## `radial-gradient(rgba(15,27,45,.075) 1.1px, transparent 1.3px)` at
  ## `background-size 22px 22px`, i.e. one 1.1px dot per 22px tile over the whole
  ## 860x756 area (40 x 35 = 1,400 tiles). That never changes, so it is rasterised
  ## once into a Layer and blitted, rather than re-issued every frame.
  if not dotBaked:
    dotField = newLayer(int(VW), int(VH))
    dotBaked = true
  else:
    dotField.clear()
  var t = Draw()
  ## html:13/18 — the dot ink is theme-dependent, so the field is re-baked on
  ## every theme toggle (applyTheme calls this again).
  let dc = if darkTheme: rgba(231, 237, 246, 0.06'f32)
           else: rgba(15, 27, 45, 0.075'f32)
  var ty = 11
  while ty < int(VH):
    var tx = 11
    while tx < int(VW):
      t = t.circle(float32(tx), float32(ty), 1.1'f32, dc)
      tx += 22
    ty += 22
  dotField.draw(t)

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ PAGE CHROME
## Everything above draws the 3D scene. Everything below draws the DOM the source
## wraps AROUND that canvas (html:29-66 styles, html:146-228 markup). In the
## browser this is a node tree that lays itself out; here the whole page is one
## `Draw` list, so every box and every string is positioned by hand.
##
## ## WHERE THE NUMBERS COME FROM
## Every geometry constant in this block is an INK BOUNDING BOX measured out of
## `.board/frames/ref052b.png` with `.board/refink.mjs` (ink rows per band) and
## `.board/refwpos.mjs` (ink runs per column), not a value read off the
## stylesheet — the stylesheet gives the box, the frame gives the ink. Two
## elements disagree between the two; those are marked `note:` and the frame wins,
## because the frame is what the diff is taken against.

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────── measured chrome geometry
const
  PAN_L  = 887.0'f32   ## .panel content left: the 1px border at x=860 + padding-left 26
  PAN_R  = 1254.0'f32  ## .panel content right: 1280 - padding-right 26
  ANN_X  = 923.0'f32   ## `.ann` body column left: PAN_L + 8 pad + 24 pin + 10 gap,
                       ## nudged +1 from the CSS sum — see `panelContent`, which
                       ## registers every run of ink against the reference.
  ANN_X3 = 924.0'f32   ## the `.ann h3` column sits 1px right of the body column
  ANN_W  = 313.0'f32   ## that column's width, (PAN_R-PAN_L) - 18 - 36
  TXT_BL = 1.0833'f32 ## MEASURED: `Draw.text`'s y is the TOP OF THE PARAGRAPH LINE
                       ## BOX (libs/nimskia/src/skia_shim.cpp:291 paints with
                       ## SkParagraph::paint(x, y)), not the baseline the doc comment
                       ## at libs/nimskia/src/nimskia.nim:76 claims. baseline = y +
                       ## 1.0833 * size — see .board/reports/ISSUES.md E1. HTML puts
                       ## every baseline on the alphabetic baseline, so `txt` below
                       ## takes the BASELINE and does the subtraction, and every
                       ## call site states a measured baseline.

proc txt(d: var Draw; x, baseline: float32; size: float32; c: uint32;
         s: string; weight = 400) =
  ## One line of text with its BASELINE at `baseline`. See TXT_BL above.
  d = d.text(x, baseline - TXT_BL * size, size, c, s, weight)

proc rrect(d: var Draw; x, y, w, h, r, inset: float32; border, fill: uint32) =
  ## A rounded rectangle with a 1px border. `Draw.rect` (sdk/ui/paint.nim:57) takes a
  ## radius but only FILLS, and there is no `strokeRect`, so the border is an outer
  ## fill plus an inset fill. Used by `.ctrls`, the theme button and `.fact`.
  if inset > 0.0'f32: d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, border, r)
  else: d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, fill, r)
  if inset > 0.0'f32:
    d = d.rect(x + inset, y + inset, w - inset * 2.0'f32, h - inset * 2.0'f32,
               fill, max(0.0'f32, r - inset))

# ────────────────────────────────────────────── the missing measurement API
proc adv(c: char): int =
  ## Advance width of `c` in 1/1000 em for the source's UI face.
  ## ⚠️ INFERRED — the SDK has NO way to ask how wide a string will render
  ## (`sdk/ui/paint.nim:63` returns `Draw` and nothing else; the native side does
  ## have `measureText`, see ISSUES.md E6). `wrapText` therefore needs a table of
  ## its own. FITTED, NOT GUESSED: calibrated against word widths measured in
  ## ref052b.png — at 13px "The"=21 "strands"=41 "are"=17 "antiparallel."=65; at
  ## 15px "together"=57 "around"=46 "every"=35 "turn."=30 "paired"=40 "to"=13.
  ## Every one of those lands within ~1px of the measurement.
  result = case c
  of ' ': 232
  of 'a': 500
  of 'b': 546
  of 'c': 470
  of 'd': 546
  of 'e': 514
  of 'f': 306
  of 'g': 530
  of 'h': 549
  of 'i': 225
  of 'j': 225
  of 'k': 486
  of 'l': 225
  of 'm': 807
  of 'n': 549
  of 'o': 538
  of 'p': 546
  of 'q': 546
  of 'r': 341
  of 's': 462
  of 't': 326
  of 'u': 549
  of 'v': 470
  of 'w': 733
  of 'x': 470
  of 'y': 470
  of 'z': 418
  of 'A': 608
  of 'B': 595
  of 'C': 623
  of 'D': 646
  of 'E': 535
  of 'F': 514
  of 'G': 671
  of 'H': 660
  of 'I': 263
  of 'J': 263
  of 'K': 597
  of 'L': 504
  of 'M': 793
  of 'N': 679
  of 'O': 700
  of 'P': 579
  of 'Q': 700
  of 'R': 595
  of 'S': 551
  of 'T': 544
  of 'U': 639
  of 'V': 583
  of 'W': 861
  of 'X': 574
  of 'Y': 535
  of 'Z': 538
  of '0'..'9': 575
  of '.', ',', ';', ':': 232
  of '-': 340
  of '=': 600
  of '(', ')': 340
  of '\'': 230
  else: 500
  ## NB: only ASCII. The page also uses '·' U+00B7, '′' U+2032, '→' U+2192,
  ## '≡' U+2261, '–' U+2013 and '≈' U+2248, and a Nim `char` literal is ONE BYTE,
  ## so `of '·'` does not compile ("missing closing '"). They are priced in
  ## `textW` below by their UTF-8 lead byte instead.

proc textW(s: string; size: float32): float32 =
  ## Width of `s` in px, on the same per-1000-em table `adv` uses.
  ## Non-ASCII is priced by the UTF-8 LEAD BYTE: text crosses into the engine as
  ## bytes and Nim iterates bytes, so every continuation byte (0x80..0xBF) counts
  ## zero and the lead byte carries the whole glyph. 0xC2 opens '·' (300), 0xE2
  ## opens the five 3-byte glyphs (800 each).
  var i = 0
  while i < s.len:
    let b = int(ord(s[i]))
    if b < 0x80:
      result += float32(adv(s[i])) * size / 1000.0'f32
      inc i
    else:
      result += (if b == 0xC2: 300 else: 800).float32 * size / 1000.0'f32
      while i < s.len and int(ord(s[i])) >= 0x80: inc i

proc wrapText(s: string; size, maxW: float32): seq[string] =
  ## Greedy line breaking, the job CSS `text-wrap: pretty` does for `.ann p`
  ## (html:78). `Draw.text` cannot wrap — it is one `T` command per string.
  ## Breaks on spaces, never mid-word, exactly as the browser did: verified
  ## against the title paragraph, where this reproduces all three measured lines.
  var line = ""
  var word = ""
  for c in s:
    if c == ' ':
      if line.len > 0 and textW(line & " " & word, size) > maxW:
        result.add line
        line = word
      else:
        line = line & " " & word
      word = ""
    else:
      word.add c
  if line.len > 0: result.add line
  if word.len > 0:
    if result.len > 0: result[result.len - 1] = result[result.len - 1] & " " & word
    else: result.add word

proc lines(d: var Draw; x, firstBaseline: float32; size, leading: float32;
           c: uint32; s: string; maxW: float32; weight = 400) =
  ## Draw `s` wrapped to `maxW`, one `Draw.text` per line, `leading` apart.
  let ls = wrapText(s, size, maxW)
  for i in 0 ..< ls.len:
    txt(d, x, firstBaseline + float32(i) * leading, size, c, ls[i], weight)

const BOLD_ADV = 1.12'f32
  ## MEASURED: `adv` above is a REGULAR-weight advance table, but the engine's only
  ## family (Segoe UI, libs/nimskia/src/skia_shim.cpp:142) has a real Bold whose
  ## glyphs are wider. With `.2em` tracking applied correctly, this port's
  ## `.kicker` still spanned x35..248 where ref052b.png spans x35..267 — 19px short
  ## across 18 advances, i.e. 1.06px a step against a mean advance of ~8.9px =
  ## 1.12x. That ratio belongs to the typeface, not to this string, so it is
  ## applied to every run at weight >= 600.

proc tracked(d: var Draw; x, baseline: float32; size: float32; c: uint32;
             s: string; track: float32; weight = 400): float32 =
  ## A run carrying `letter-spacing`, which `Draw.text` cannot express
  ## (ISSUES.md E1: it takes no tracking argument). Emitted one glyph per command,
  ## each at its own x, advancing by `adv` — scaled by BOLD_ADV at weight >= 600 —
  ## plus `track`. That makes `.kicker`'s .2em (html:49), `.panel h2`'s .18em
  ## (html:70) and `.title h1`'s -.035em (html:50) drawable at all.
  ##
  ## RETURNS the pen x after the run. The port cannot colour part of a string, so
  ## `The Double <span>Helix</span>` is two runs, and the second one's x must come
  ## from the first the way the browser lays it out — a hard-coded constant there
  ## goes stale the moment the tracking is reproduced.
  ##
  ## UTF-8 is walked by lead byte, exactly as `textW` prices it, so the multi-byte
  ## glyphs this page uses ('·' U+00B7, '→' U+2192, '≡' U+2261, '≈' U+2248) stay
  ## one glyph costing one advance, not one per byte — a Nim `char` is one byte,
  ## so `for c in s` would emit a glyph and an advance for each.
  let scale = (if weight >= 600: BOLD_ADV else: 1.0'f32)
  var cx = x
  var i = 0
  while i < s.len:
    let b = int(ord(s[i]))
    var step = 1
    var aw: float32
    if b < 0x80:
      aw = float32(adv(s[i])) * scale * size / 1000.0'f32
    else:
      while i + step < s.len and int(ord(s[i + step])) >= 0x80: inc step
      aw = (if b == 0xC2: 300 else: 800).float32 * size / 1000.0'f32
    if s[i] != ' ':
      d = d.text(cx, baseline - TXT_BL * size, size, c, s[i ..< i + step], weight)
    cx += aw + track
    i += step
  cx

# ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── `.top` (html:30)
proc header(d: var Draw) =
  ## html:30, 147-158 — the 60px white bar: the DNA mark, the wordmark, the
  ## strapline and the theme button. ref052b.png ink: mark x25..45 y18..41,
  ## "Helix" x59..99 baseline 37, strapline x131..284 baseline 35, theme button
  ## x1220..1257 y11..48 with the sun glyph at x1230..1248.
  ## `.mark svg` (html:149) is 26x26 at padding-left 22, centred in the 60px bar.
  d = d.svgPath("M5 2 C5 9 21 9 21 13 C21 17 5 17 5 24", 22, 17, 1.0'f32, C_TEAL, 2.4)
  d = d.svgPath("M21 2 C21 9 5 9 5 13 C5 17 21 17 21 24", 22, 17, 1.0'f32, C_RED, 2.4)
  d = d.svgPath("M8 5.5H18M8 20.5H18", 22, 17, 1.0'f32, C_A, 1.8)
  ## `.mark b` (html:33) — 18px / 700, letter-spacing -.02em (not reproducible:
  ## `Draw.text` has no tracking, see E1).
  txt(d, 58.0'f32, 37.0'f32, 18.0'f32, C_INK, "Helix", 700)
  ## `.top .tag` (html:34, 152) — 1px left border at x=114, 13px text after 14px padding.
  d = d.line(114.0'f32, 23.0'f32, 114.0'f32, 37.0'f32, C_LINE, 1.0'f32)
  txt(d, 130.0'f32, 35.0'f32, 13.0'f32, C_INK3, "The double helix, explained")
  ## `.iconbtn#themeBtn` (html:36, 154-157) — 38x38 at the bar's right padding.
  rrect(d, 1220.0'f32, 11.0'f32, 38.0'f32, 38.0'f32, 10.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL)
  if darkTheme:
    ## the moon icon (html:156) in the same 18x18 box.
    d = d.svgPath("M16.5 12.4A7 7 0 0 1 7.6 3.5a7 7 0 1 0 8.9 8.9Z",
                  1230.0'f32, 21.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK2, 1.7)
  else:
    ## the sun icon (html:155) in an 18x18 box at (1230,21): viewBox 20 scaled 0.9.
    d = d.svgPath("M10 1.8v2M10 16.2v2M1.8 10h2M16.2 10h2M4.2 4.2l1.4 1.4M14.4 14.4l1.4 1.4M4.2 15.8l1.4-1.4M14.4 5.6l1.4-1.4",
                  1230.0'f32, 21.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK2, 1.7)
    ## `<circle cx=10 cy=10 r=3.6>` is stroked in CSS but `Draw.circle` only fills,
    ## so the ring is a filled disc with a smaller disc punched back out in --panel.
    d = d.circle(1239.0'f32, 30.0'f32, 4.0'f32, C_INK2)
    d = d.circle(1239.0'f32, 30.0'f32, 2.48'f32, C_PANEL)

# ──────────────────────────────── `.title` (html:48-52) + `.stage-cap` (html:59)
proc vizTitle(d: var Draw) =
  ## html:48-52, 163-166 — the overlay block at the top-left of `.viz`.
  ## ref052b.png ink: kicker x35..267 rows 108..118; "The Double" x34..277 and
  ## "Helix" x283..362, cap top 143; the paragraph three lines ending at x383,
  ## x389 and x63.
  ##
  ## `.stage-cap` (html:59-61) is NOT drawn. MEASURED: ref052b.png x676..836
  ## y84..121 is 99.1% page background and the darkest pixel anywhere in the
  ## viz's top-right quadrant (x500..860 y60..180) has luminance 171.5, which is
  ## helix antialiasing. There is no pill in the frame. That is correct: the
  ## element ships with `opacity:0` (html:60) and only `.stage-cap.on` shows it
  ## (html:61), and `on` is added by `caption()` from `startReplication()`
  ## (html:660), which no click ever fires before the capture. Drawing it would
  ## ADD error, so it is omitted on purpose, not overlooked.
  ##
  ## ## the kicker: 15px / --ink2, and the reason is the CASCADE, not a stale
  ## ## capture. `.kicker` is a `<p>` (html:164) sitting inside `.title`, so
  ## ## `.title p` (html:52) matches it too and wins on specificity — (0,1,1)
  ## ## beats (0,1,0) — carrying `margin-top:12px; font-size:15px; line-height:1.5;
  ## ## color:var(--ink2); max-width:25em`. `.kicker` therefore keeps only
  ## ## `font-weight:700`, `letter-spacing:.2em` and `text-transform:uppercase`;
  ## ## its own `font-size:11px` and `color:var(--teal)` are both overridden.
  ## ## This retires the `check:` that stood here, which read the disagreement with
  ## ## html:49 as a capture artefact. It is not: BOTH references agree to the
  ## ## pixel (ref052.png and ref052b.png, rows 108..118, x35..267, identical), and
  ## ## both match the cascade — caps span 0.727 x 15 = 10.9 rows, measured 11,
  ## ## where 11px would be 8; and the darkest pixel is (70,86,107) = --ink2 at
  ## ## luminance 83.6, where --teal would be 149.4.
  ## ## `letter-spacing` is what finally takes this line out to the reference's
  ## ## x267, via `tracked`.
  discard tracked(d, 34.0'f32, 119.0'f32, 15.0'f32, C_INK2, "B-DNA · RIGHT-HANDED",
                  0.2'f32 * 15.0'f32, 700)
  ## `.title h1` (html:50-51) — `clamp(30px,3.6vw,52px)` = 46.08px at 1280 wide.
  ## `letter-spacing:-.035em` (html:50) = -1.613px a glyph at this size, so both
  ## halves go through `tracked`; and the `<span>` in --ink3 is a SECOND run whose
  ## x is the first run's return value. It used to be a hard-coded 287, which was
  ## correct only while the tracking was missing — reproducing the tracking moved
  ## where "The Double " ends, and the constant would have left a 40px hole.
  ## Weight is 700, not html:50's 800: with Segoe UI as the only family 800
  ## resolves to Black, and the reference's 800 is nowhere near that heavy.
  let hx = tracked(d, 33.0'f32, 177.0'f32, 46.08'f32, C_INK, "The Double ",
                   -0.035'f32 * 46.08'f32, 700)
  discard tracked(d, hx, 177.0'f32, 46.08'f32, C_INK3, "Helix",
                  -0.035'f32 * 46.08'f32, 300)
  ## `.title p` (html:52, 166) — 15px / --ink2, `max-width:25em` = 375px. The three
  ## lines are the MEASURED wrap (ink right edges 383, 389, 63), drawn as three
  ## strings rather than through `lines`, because the engine's metrics are not the
  ## browser's and the measured breaks are the ground truth. Line 3's baseline is
  ## 254.5, not 256: "turn." has no ascender above cap height, so the measured ink
  ## top of row 245 is a 0.727em cap line, and the reference's rows are 198/221/245
  ## — a 22.5px pitch, matching html:52's line-height 1.5.
  txt(d, 34.0'f32, 209.0'f32, 15.0'f32, C_INK2, "Two antiparallel strands wound around one axis, held")
  txt(d, 34.0'f32, 232.0'f32, 15.0'f32, C_INK2, "together by paired bases, ten and a half pairs to every")
  txt(d, 34.0'f32, 254.5'f32, 15.0'f32, C_INK2, "turn.")

# ───────────────────────────── `.ctrls` (html:53-57) + `.hint` (html:58, 179)
proc vizChrome(d: var Draw) =
  ## ref052b.png ink: the `.ctrls` pill is x28..201 y742..789 (174x48, radius 14,
  ## --panel fill, --line border); `.hint` line 1 x674..833 rows 751..761,
  ## line 2 x691..833 rows 769..779.
  ## `.ctrls` (html:53-54, 170-178) — padding 5 + 1px border, four 36x36
  ## `.iconbtn`s at x34 / 76 / 118 / 160, each with an 18x18 svg centred in it.
  rrect(d, 28.0'f32, 742.0'f32, 174.0'f32, 48.0'f32, 14.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL)
  ## html:55-57 — pause bars while auto-rotating, the play triangle when paused.
  if autoRot:
    d = d.svgPath("M7 4.5v11M13 4.5v11", 43.0'f32, 757.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK, 1.7) # pause
  else:
    d = d.svgPath("M6.5 4.2L15.5 10L6.5 15.8L6.5 4.2", 43.0'f32, 757.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK, 1.7) # play
  d = d.svgPath("M4.5 10h11", 85.0'f32, 757.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK, 1.7)            # zoom out
  d = d.svgPath("M4.5 10h11M10 4.5v11", 127.0'f32, 757.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK, 1.7) # zoom in
  d = d.svgPath("M4 9a6 6 0 1 1 1.8 4.6M4 4.5V9h4.5", 169.0'f32, 757.0'f32, 0.9'f32, C_INK, 1.7) # reset
  ## `.hint` (html:58, 179) — 12px --ink3, `right:26px` so the text's right edge
  ## lands at 834, `text-align:right`. Right-alignment is an ESTIMATE: `adv` says
  ## these two strings are 167.8px and 156.6px wide at 12px. Measured after the
  ## first build, this port's hint ink ran x684..841 where the reference's runs
  ## x674..833, so `adv` over-states this face's advance by 8px at 12px and the
  ## right margin below is pulled in by the same 8px. The remaining 1px — this
  ## port's runs sit at 676/684/688/694 against the reference's 675/683/688/694
  ## — is taken out too, which is what the 826 below encodes.
  let h1 = "Drag to rotate · Scroll to zoom"
  let h2 = "Hover a base pair to read it"
  txt(d, 825.0'f32 - textW(h1, 12.0'f32), 760.0'f32, 12.0'f32, C_INK3, h1)
  txt(d, 825.0'f32 - textW(h2, 12.0'f32), 778.0'f32, 12.0'f32, C_INK3, h2)

# ────────────────────────────────────────────────── the annotation panel (html:69-118)
const
  ANN_H3 = ["5′ → 3′ direction", "Sugar–phosphate backbone", "Major groove",
            "Minor groove", "Hydrogen bonds"]
  ## html:185-189 — the five `.ann h3` headings, in DOM order.
  ANN_BODY = [
    "The strands are antiparallel. The teal strand runs 5′→3′ along the axis; its red partner runs 5′→3′ the opposite way. The small chevrons show each strand's direction.",
    "Each strand's outer rail: deoxyribose sugars and phosphate groups joined by phosphodiester bonds. The phosphates give DNA its negative charge.",
    "The wide gap, about 2.2 nm across. The edges of the bases are most exposed here, so many DNA-binding proteins read the sequence through it.",
    "The narrow gap, about 1.2 nm across. The grooves differ because the two backbones are not directly opposite each other around the axis.",
    "Adenine pairs with thymine through two hydrogen bonds, and guanine with cytosine through three. A purine always faces a pyrimidine, so every rung is the same width."]
  ## html:185-189 — the five `.ann p` bodies, in DOM order.
  ANN_PIN_Y = [121.0'f32, 227.0'f32, 332.0'f32, 438.0'f32, 543.0'f32]
  ## MEASURED `.pin` top edges in ref052b.png — each a 24x24 circle at x887..910.
  ANN_H3_BASE = [136.0'f32, 243.0'f32, 347.0'f32, 457.0'f32, 559.0'f32]
  ## MEASURED `.ann h3` baselines: cap top (125/232/336/446/548) + 0.727 * 14.5.
  ANN_HOT_H = [104.0'f32, 103.0'f32, 104.0'f32, 103.0'f32, 112.0'f32]
  ## hovered-row wash heights: pin top - 10 to the next row's wash top, so the
  ## washes tile exactly like the DOM boxes (html:73).
  ANN_P_BASE = [159.5'f32, 264.5'f32, 369.5'f32, 475.5'f32, 580.5'f32]
  ## MEASURED first `.ann p` baseline per entry, +1 off the reference-derived
  ## value: this port's 13px body lands one row high against ref052b.png on every
  ## line of every entry (reference rows 149-161 / 168-181 / 187-200, this port's
  ## 148-160 / 167-180 / 186-199), and text registered to the wrong row differs on
  ## every antialiased edge. Line pitch MEASURED at 19.2 from rows 149/168/187.
  ## ANN_X is likewise +1: this port's body column starts one pixel left of the
  ## reference's on all three lines (922 vs 923), and `.ann h3` two pixels left
  ## (923 vs 925). Everything else — `.panel h2`, `.fact b`, `.fact span`, the
  ## header wordmark, `.hint` — already lands on the reference's pixels.

proc panelContent(d: var Draw) =
  ## html:69-118, 182-225 — the right-hand column. Only what the 816px window
  ## shows is drawn: `.panel` is `overflow-y:auto` (html:69), and ref052b.png
  ## ends part-way through the second `.fact`, so "Build a sequence", the seq
  ## strip, the Legend and the footnote all start below y=816 and are off-frame
  ## in the reference too.
  ##
  ## `.panel h2` (html:70-71) — 11px / 700 / uppercase / --ink3 / letter-spacing
  ## .18em = 1.98px a glyph, so these go through `tracked` like the kicker.
  ## MEASURED: "ANATOMY" x887..953 rows 88..95 (baseline 96),
  ## "MEASUREMENTS" x887..996 rows 684..691 (baseline 692).
  discard tracked(d, PAN_L, 96.0'f32, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, "ANATOMY",
                  0.18'f32 * 11.0'f32, 700)
  discard tracked(d, PAN_L, 692.0'f32, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, "MEASUREMENTS",
                  0.18'f32 * 11.0'f32, 700)
  ##
  ## `.anns` (html:184-190) — five `.ann` rows. Each is `.pin` (html:75, a 24px
  ## --ink disc with a white number), an `.ann h3` (html:77, 14.5px / 650) and an
  ## `.ann p` (html:78, 13px / --ink2 / line-height 1.5) wrapped to ANN_W.
  ## The h3's weight is 600, not html:77's 650: Segoe UI's installed faces step
  ## 400 -> 600 -> 700, so 650 has to land on one side or the other, and at 650
  ## this port carried 331 dark pixels per line against the reference's 208 — 60%
  ## too much ink. 600 is the nearest face the engine can actually draw.
  for i in 0 .. 4:
    ## html:73-76 — the hovered row gets a --panel2 wash and its pin goes
    ## --focus (cold rows are untouched, so the static frame is unchanged).
    if annHot == i:
      rrect(d, 879.0'f32, ANN_PIN_Y[i] - 10.0'f32, 383.0'f32,
            ANN_HOT_H[i], 12.0'f32, 0.0'f32, C_PANEL2, C_PANEL2)
    d = d.circle(898.5'f32, ANN_PIN_Y[i] + 12.0'f32, 12.0'f32,
                 (if annHot == i: C_FOCUS else: C_INK))
    txt(d, 894.5'f32, ANN_PIN_Y[i] + 17.0'f32, 12.0'f32, C_BG, $char(ord('1') + i), 700)
    txt(d, ANN_X3, ANN_H3_BASE[i], 14.5'f32, C_INK, ANN_H3[i], 600)
    lines(d, ANN_X, ANN_P_BASE[i], 13.0'f32, 19.2'f32, C_INK2, ANN_BODY[i], ANN_W)
  ##
  ## the two `.bond` pills inside the fifth h3 (html:79, 189) — 11.5px mono on
  ## --panel2 with a --line border. MEASURED: x1043..1099 and x1108..1165,
  ## both y544..561 (this port's pill borders land on exactly those rows), their
  ## text on baseline 558 — one lower than the reference-derived 557, because at
  ## 557 this port's ink ran rows 548..556 where the reference's runs 549..556.
  rrect(d, 1043.0'f32, 544.0'f32, 57.0'f32, 18.0'f32, 6.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  txt(d, 1050.0'f32, 558.0'f32, 11.5'f32, C_INK2, "A=T · 2")
  rrect(d, 1108.0'f32, 544.0'f32, 58.0'f32, 18.0'f32, 6.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  txt(d, 1115.0'f32, 558.0'f32, 11.5'f32, C_INK2, "G≡C · 3")
  ##
  ## `.facts` (html:193-198) — a `1fr 1fr` grid with an 8px gap, so it is TWO
  ## COLUMNS, not a stack. MEASURED in ref052b.png: row 1 is x887..1066 and
  ## x1075..1253, both y707..775; row 2 is both x ranges again at y784..852, which
  ## the 816px window cuts 36px short of. (A first pass here read the row-2 band as
  ## a second row rather than a second COLUMN and put one box at (1075,784) — that
  ## alone left 134 of every 160 pixels in x1120..1280 y714..775 differing.)
  ## `.fact b` (html:82) is 19px/700/--ink, cap top 726 -> baseline 739;
  ## `.fact span` (html:83) is 11.5px/--ink2, ascender top 752 -> baseline 761.
  rrect(d, 887.0'f32, 707.0'f32, 180.0'f32, 69.0'f32, 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  txt(d, 899.0'f32, 739.0'f32, 19.0'f32, C_INK, "10.5", 700)
  txt(d, 899.0'f32, 761.0'f32, 11.5'f32, C_INK2, "base pairs per turn")
  rrect(d, 1075.0'f32, 707.0'f32, 179.0'f32, 69.0'f32, 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  ## CORRECTED 2026-10-04. This box was "0.22 nm" / "major groove width" and the
  ## note here used to argue that "the frame wins". That inference was INVALID:
  ## the evidence was the ink RUN STRUCTURE at rows 724..740 (two runs, x1088..1123
  ## and x1130..1156, no leading gap), which shows only that there is no leading
  ## "≈ ". It cannot distinguish the digits, because "0.22" and "0.34" have the
  ## same four glyphs in the same places and the same run structure.
  ## The caption settles it outright. Measured caption ink in ref052b.png at
  ## y=757 spans x1088..1172 = 85 px. With this file's own `adv` table at 11.5px
  ## (`.board/dna52/` measurements):
  ##     "rise per base pair"  -> 83.6 px   <-- the 85 px measured
  ##     "major groove width"  -> 94.1 px   <-- what the port rendered, 98 px on screen
  ## So the reference shows html:195 verbatim. No JS rewrites `.facts` (the only
  ## `textContent =` writes in the page target the sequence message and the
  ## caption), so the markup is authoritative and the port was simply wrong.
  ## "major/minor groove width" are not facts at all: they live in the `.ann`
  ## annotation list (html:187-188), and a previous pass pulled them in here.
  txt(d, 1087.0'f32, 739.0'f32, 19.0'f32, C_INK, "0.34 nm", 700)
  txt(d, 1087.0'f32, 761.0'f32, 11.5'f32, C_INK2, "rise per base pair")
  rrect(d, 887.0'f32, 784.0'f32, 180.0'f32, 69.0'f32, 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  txt(d, 899.0'f32, 816.0'f32, 19.0'f32, C_INK, "≈ 3.6 nm", 700)
  txt(d, 899.0'f32, 838.0'f32, 11.5'f32, C_INK2, "length of one turn")
  rrect(d, 1075.0'f32, 784.0'f32, 179.0'f32, 69.0'f32, 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  txt(d, 1087.0'f32, 816.0'f32, 19.0'f32, C_INK, "≈ 2 nm", 700)
  txt(d, 1087.0'f32, 838.0'f32, 11.5'f32, C_INK2, "helix diameter")

# ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ CANVAS OVERLAY (html:454-584)
## Everything `draw()` paints AFTER the sorted item pool, plus the SVG leader
## lines. None of it can live in the pool: it is screen-space annotation, not
## depth-sorted geometry, and html emits it in a fixed order around the loop.
##
## MEASURED against ref052b.png (1280x816, wait:8). The five pin centres, by
## connected-component bbox of the pure --ink disc
## (.board/dna-ov/pinscan.mjs, restricted to the canvas rect):
##   (643.5,507.0) (220.5,528.5) (224.5,566.0) (364.5,516.0) (194.0,519.0)
## The radial profile at y=507 (`.board/dna-ov/slice.mjs`) is pure --ink out to
## r=8.5, a white band over 8.5..10.5, then background — which is exactly
## html:553-554, `fill()` to r=9.5 followed by a `lineWidth:2` white `stroke()`
## straddling that arc. Hence OV_PIN_R +/- OV_RING_W/2, not a painted halo.

const
  OV_PIN_R    = 9.5'f32     ## html:550 `r = (hot?12:9.5) * (landscape?1:.86)`;
                            ## `landscape` is true at 1280x816 (frameGeometry)
  OV_RING_W   = 2.0'f32     ## html:554 stroke width
  OV_DIGIT_PX = 11.0'f32    ## html:545 `700 11px` SANS
  OV_MID_EM   = 0.586'f32   ## canvas `textBaseline:'middle'` sits this far ABOVE
                            ## the baseline. ⚠️ INFERRED — Chrome places 'middle'
                            ## at half the em square and the SDK exposes no metric
                            ## API, so this is a DejaVu-class guess. Corrected by
                            ## measurement, see apps/dna/overlay-notes.md.
const OV_WHITE = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32

type Tgt = object
  ok: bool
  x, y, d: float32          ## d = PD, the depth frontMost ranks by

var ovT: array[6, Tgt]
const
  OV_DIR = 0
  OV_BB = 1
  OV_MAJ = 2
  OV_MIN = 3
  OV_HB0 = 4
  OV_HB1 = 5

proc surfOv(ii: float32; ang, rad: float32): Tgt =
  ## html:528. `ii` is fractional because 'maj'/'min' sample the half-base
  ## `i + .5` (html:533-534).
  let th = TWIST * ii + spin + ang
  let u = (ii - float32(n() - 1) / 2.0'f32) * RISE
  proj(u, rad * cos(th), -rad * sin(th))
  Tgt(ok: true, x: PX, y: PY, d: PD)

proc frontMost(lo, hi: float32; fn: proc (i: int32): Tgt): Tgt =
  ## html:522-527. `a = max(0, floor(n*lo))`, `b = min(n-1, ceil(n*hi))`, and the
  ## winner is the greatest PD in that window — the point nearest the camera.
  let nn = n()
  let a = max(0'i32, int32(floor(float32(nn) * lo)))
  let b = min(nn - 1, int32(ceil(float32(nn) * hi)))
  var best: Tgt
  var bd = -1e9'f32
  var i = a
  while i <= b:
    let r = fn(i)
    if r.ok and r.d > bd:
      bd = r.d
      best = r
    inc i
  best

proc pairAtOv(want: bool): Tgt =
  ## html:535-541. The H-bond midpoint: at A/T pairs when `want`, else G/C.
  frontMost(0.02'f32, 0.5'f32, proc (i: int32): Tgt =
    let at = seqStr[i] == 'A' or seqStr[i] == 'T'
    if want != at: return Tgt()
    let th = TWIST * float32(i) + spin
    let u = (float32(i) - float32(n() - 1) / 2.0'f32) * RISE
    let f = (if PUR[baseIdx(seqStr[i])]: 0.545'f32 else: 0.455'f32)
    let xv = RB * cos(th)
    let xw = -RB * sin(th)
    let pv = RB * cos(th + DELTA)
    let pw = -RB * sin(th + DELTA)
    proj(u, xv + (pv - xv) * f, xw + (pw - xw) * f)
    Tgt(ok: true, x: PX, y: PY, d: PD))

proc computeTargetsOv() =
  ## html:529-542.
  ovT[OV_DIR] = frontMost(0.8'f32, 1.0'f32, proc (i: int32): Tgt =
    surfOv(float32(i), 0.0'f32, RAD))
  ovT[OV_BB] = frontMost(0.6'f32, 0.8'f32, proc (i: int32): Tgt =
    surfOv(float32(i), DELTA, RAD))
  ovT[OV_MAJ] = frontMost(0.42'f32, 0.62'f32, proc (i: int32): Tgt =
    surfOv(float32(i) + 0.5'f32, PI + DELTA / 2.0'f32, RAD * 0.96'f32))
  ovT[OV_MIN] = frontMost(0.24'f32, 0.44'f32, proc (i: int32): Tgt =
    surfOv(float32(i) + 0.5'f32, DELTA / 2.0'f32, RAD * 0.92'f32))
  ovT[OV_HB0] = pairAtOv(true)
  ovT[OV_HB1] = pairAtOv(false)

proc drawPinsOv(d: var Draw) =
  ## html:544-560. `annHot` is null and `annAlpha` is 1 at capture time (no click
  ## fires before the screenshot), so every pin is the cold colour and fully
  ## opaque. `ctx.fillText(String(idx+1), ...)` numbers them in ANN order, so
  ## the two `hb` pins both carry "5".
