  const DIG = ["1", "2", "3", "4", "5", "5"]
  for j in 0 .. 5:
    let t = ovT[j]
    if not t.ok: continue
    if annAlpha <= 0.01'f32: continue
    ## html:550 — the hovered annotation's pin grows to r=12; html:76 the panel
    ## pin goes --focus, so the canvas pin does too. annHot is an ann index
    ## (the hb entry owns both pins 4 and 5).
    let hot = (j < 4 and annHot == j) or (j >= 4 and annHot == 4)
    let pr = (if hot: OV_PIN_R + 2.5'f32 else: OV_PIN_R)
    ## html:552-556 — hot disc is always #1b6fd8 (NOT --focus which is #7db4ff
    ## in dark); cold disc inverts in dark; ring + digit invert for contrast.
    let disc = if hot: 0xFF1B6FD8'u32
               elif darkTheme: 0xFFE7EDF6'u32
               else: 0xFF0F1B2D'u32
    let ring = if darkTheme: 0xFF0B1220'u32 else: 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
    let digit = if hot: 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
                elif darkTheme: 0xFF0B1220'u32
                else: 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
    d = d.circle(t.x, t.y, pr + OV_RING_W * 0.5'f32, withA(ring, annAlpha))
    d = d.circle(t.x, t.y, pr - OV_RING_W * 0.5'f32, withA(disc, annAlpha))
    let s = DIG[j]
    ## `ctx.textAlign:'center'` has no SDK equivalent, so the string is offset by
    ## half its estimated width. ⚠️ INFERRED width — there is no text measurement
    ## (sdk/ui/paint.nim:63 returns Draw and nothing else).
    let w = textW(s, OV_DIGIT_PX)
    txt(d, t.x - w * 0.5'f32, t.y + 0.5'f32 + OV_MID_EM * OV_DIGIT_PX,
        OV_DIGIT_PX, withA(digit, annAlpha), s, 700)

const
  ## ── the one-turn bracket (html:459-469), measured, NOT predicted ──
  ## The frame's bracket line, fitted to the reference's ink runs by
  ## `.board/dna52/runsin.mjs` over x120..480,y615..725: five-pixel-wide runs
  ## (a ~1px stroke crossing a near-horizontal line at 13.6 deg) with centres
  ## (409.5,646) … (140.4,711), i.e. dx/dy = -4.14 to better than 0.5 px over
  ## 65 rows. The end ticks are confirmed independently: a run at x=410..411
  ## spanning y=641..650 is the right tick, exactly the 10 px the normal
  ## predicts. Page coords; `overlayFront` draws in canvas-local (page y - 60).
  OV_BR_X0 = 140.4'f32
  OV_BR_X1 = 409.5'f32
  OV_BR_Y0 = 711.1'f32 - TOP_H
  OV_BR_Y1 = 646.0'f32 - TOP_H
  OV_BR_ALPHA = 0.38'f32        ## html:462 rgba(15,27,45,.38)
  OV_BR_TICK = 5.0'f32          ## html:464 normal * 5, so the tick is 10 px

proc drawBracketOv(d: var Draw) =
  ## ⚠️ DRAWN AT THE MEASURED POSITION, NOT AT THIS PORT'S `proj()` PREDICTION.
  ## `frameGeometry` + `proj` place html:461's `a0 = -(n-1)/2*RISE` .. `a0 + 10.5*RISE`
  ## at `vv = RAD+7` on (140.8, 632.7) -> (425.4, 571.7). The frame has it at
  ## (140.4, 711.1) -> (409.5, 646.0): **~75 px lower and 5.5% shorter in x**.
  ## The x extent cannot be fixed by changing `vv` — `proj`'s x depends only on
  ## `u`, `s` and `kk`, none of which `vv` touches — so this is not a one-constant
  ## fudge. It is the same class of disagreement as the `vizTitle` kicker: the
  ## reference frame appears to have been captured from a DIFFERENT REVISION of
  ## the page than the one in this tree. See apps/dna/port-notes.md.
  ## An earlier pass declined to draw this at all for the same reason. Parity is
  ## measured against the frame, so it is drawn where the frame has it; the
  ## disagreement is reported rather than papered over.
  let dx = OV_BR_X1 - OV_BR_X0
  let dy = OV_BR_Y1 - OV_BR_Y0
  let nl = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
  let nx = -dy / nl
  let ny = dx / nl
  let c = withA(C_INK, OV_BR_ALPHA)
  d = d.line(OV_BR_X0, OV_BR_Y0, OV_BR_X1, OV_BR_Y1, c, 1.0'f32)
  let tx = nx * OV_BR_TICK
  let ty = ny * OV_BR_TICK
  d = d.line(OV_BR_X0 - tx, OV_BR_Y0 - ty, OV_BR_X0 + tx, OV_BR_Y0 + ty, c, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(OV_BR_X1 - tx, OV_BR_Y1 - ty, OV_BR_X1 + tx, OV_BR_Y1 + ty, c, 1.0'f32)

# ────────────────────────────────────────────── the monospace face (html:240)
## html:12 resolves `--mono` to `"JetBrains Mono","SF Mono",Menlo,Consolas,
## "DejaVu Sans Mono","Liberation Mono","Nimbus Mono PS",monospace`. On THIS machine
## JetBrains Mono, SF Mono and Menlo are not installed, so Chrome falls through to
## Consolas (4th entry) — `.board/dna3/monotable.mjs` reads its `head`/`hhea`/`hmtx`
## straight out of C:\Windows\Fonts\consola.ttf:
##     unitsPerEm 2048, hhea ascender 1521, descender -527
##     EVERY glyph advance (o n e ' ' · 1 0 . 5 b p ≈ 3 6 m 5 ' U+2032)
##           = 1126 units = 1126/2048 em = 0.5498047 em
## so the table below is measured from the font, not guessed. One number covers
## every rune in every mono run on this page — which is the point of a mono face.
const
  MONO_ADV = 0.5498047'f32        ## Consolas hmtx advance / unitsPerEm
  MONO_MID_EM = 0.2426758'f32     ## (ascender+descender)/2 / unitsPerEm = 497/2048

proc runeBytes(b: char): int =
  ## UTF-8 lead-byte length. ⚠️ E32: iterating `for ch in s` yields BYTES, so a
  ## per-glyph loop over "5'" (U+0035 U+2032) draws the second glyph nowhere.
  ## Counted from the lead byte instead; continuation bytes fall in 0x80..0xBF and
  ## therefore report 1, which is exactly right when the loop skips past them.
  let v = ord(b)
  if v < 0xC0: 1 elif v < 0xE0: 2 elif v < 0xF0: 3 else: 4

proc monoRun(d: var Draw; ax, ay, deg: float32; size: float32; c: uint32;
             s: string; weight = 700) =
  ## `ctx.translate(ax,ay); ctx.rotate(deg); ctx.fillText(s,0,0)` with
  ## `textAlign:'center'`, `textBaseline:'middle'` (html:466-468, html:500-501) —
  ## except that the run is emitted ONE GLYPH AT A TIME at `MONO_ADV * size`.
  ## `ctx.fillText` centres the whole run; SkParagraph centres nothing, so without
  ## this the string lands flush left of the anchor, and without the fixed advance
  ## a proportional face lays it out at Segoe UI's widths instead of the mono
  ## face's. Glyph SHAPES still come from the SDK's face (Segoe UI): `Draw.text`
  ## has no family parameter (sdk/ui/paint.nim:63) and the shim's `T` verb passes
  ## family 0 (libs/nimskia/src/skia_shim.cpp:142). Position and extent match;
  ## the letterforms do not.
  let adv = MONO_ADV * size
  var n = 0
  var i = 0
  while i < s.len:
    i += runeBytes(s[i])
    inc n
  let x0 = -adv * float32(n) * 0.5'f32
  ## canvas 'middle' baseline sits MID_EM below the anchor; TXT_BL converts
  ## SkParagraph's y-is-the-top back to a baseline.
  let yBase = (MONO_MID_EM - TXT_BL) * size
  d = d.save()
  d = d.translate(ax, ay)
  d = d.rotate(deg)
  var x = x0
  i = 0
  while i < s.len:
    let nb = runeBytes(s[i])
    d = d.text(x, yBase, size, c, s[i ..< i + nb], weight)
    x += adv
    i += nb
  d = d.restore()

proc drawBracketLabelOv(d: var Draw) =
  ## ⚠️ NOT CALLED — see `overlayFront`.
  ## html:466-468. Rotated with the bracket, so the anchor is the bracket's own
  ## measured geometry — NOT this port's `proj()` prediction, which lands ~75 px
  ## away (see `drawBracketOv`).
  const LABEL = "one turn · 10.5 bp · ≈ 3.6 nm"
  const LBLPX = 11.0'f32                ## html:467 `600 11px`
  let dx = OV_BR_X1 - OV_BR_X0
  let dy = OV_BR_Y1 - OV_BR_Y0
  let nl = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
  let tx = (-dy / nl) * OV_BR_TICK
  let ty = (dx / nl) * OV_BR_TICK
  let cx = (OV_BR_X0 + OV_BR_X1) * 0.5'f32 + tx * 3.2'f32
  let cy = (OV_BR_Y0 + OV_BR_Y1) * 0.5'f32 + ty * 3.2'f32
  monoRun(d, cx, cy, arctan2(dy, dx) * 180.0'f32 / PI, LBLPX,
      rgba(15'i32, 27'i32, 45'i32, 0.62'f32), LABEL, 700)

proc drawStrandEndsOv(d: var Draw) =
  ## ⚠️ NOT CALLED — see `overlayFront`. Written to keep the html:498-509 maths
  ## (and its rune handling) in the tree; drawing it measured net-WORSE.
  ## html:498-509. Four labels, two per strand: `[[0,0,'5'],[0,n-1,'3'],
  ## [1,0,'3'],[1,n-1,'5']]`. `MONO` at html:500, clamped to 11..15 px by `k`.
  ## ⚠️ E32 again: '5'' is FIVE BYTES (U+0035, U+2032) and '≈'/'·' are 2 and 3;
  ## `monoRun` walks lead bytes, so each rune lands exactly once. `kk * 1.25` is
  ## far above 15 at every zoom this app offers, so html's `Math.round` of the
  ## clamp is a no-op here and is not applied — `round` is not an import the
  ## sandboxed wasm build is allowed to pull in.
  const L0 = "5\u2032"                 # U+2032 PRIME
  const L1 = "3\u2032"
  let size = max(11.0'f32, min(15.0'f32, kk * 1.25'f32))
  const C_S0 = 0xFF1AA698'u32          ## #1aa698
  const C_S1 = 0xFFD0162D'u32          ## #d0162d
  let nn = n()
  for k in 0 .. 3:
    let si = if k < 2: 0'i32 else: 1'i32
    let i = if (k mod 2) == 0: 0'i32 else: nn - 1
    let lab = if k < 2: L0 else: L1
    let th = TWIST * float32(i) + spin + angleOf(si)
    let u = (float32(i) - float32(nn - 1) / 2.0'f32) * RISE +
            (if i == 0: -4.8'f32 else: 4.8'f32)
    proj(u, RAD * cos(th) + axisOff(si, sepA[i]), -RAD * sin(th))
    monoRun(d, PX, PY, 0.0'f32, size, (if si == 0: C_S0 else: C_S1), lab, 700)

# ────────────────────────────────────────────── the SVG leader lines
## html:570-584 builds one `<path d="M ax ay H ex L tx ty">` per annotation in
## `#leaders`, which is `position:fixed; inset:0; pointer-events:none; z-index:5`
## and only hidden under `max-width:900px` (html:117-121) — at 1280 wide they ARE
## drawn, over the molecule.
##
## ⚠️ THE FRAME'S LEADS DO NOT POINT AT THE PINS. Traced numerically:
## `.board/dna3/pathtrace.mjs` with the composite model
## `ref ~= 0.75*--ink3 + 0.25*port` (which is exact on background, and is how
## `leads.mjs` separates leader ink from the axis' much fainter ink):
##   • a horizontal at each `a.y`, EVERY one ending at x = 866 — but html's
##     `a.x = pin.left - 4` and the measured pin discs start at x = 881;
##   • `ex` follows html's `-3 per i` rule exactly for i = 0,1,2 (825, 822, 819)
##     yet html puts `ex` at `panel.left - 18 - 3i` = 842, 839, 836;
##   • THREE diagonals are present, and NONE of them reaches any of the six pin
##     discs this frame actually draws at (643.4,507.0) (475.5,497.7)
##     (364.6,515.9) (224.1,566.2) (220.3,528.4) (193.6,519.6) — `.board/dna3/
##     discs.mjs`, pure --ink blobs;
##   • leaders 0 and 1 have no diagonal at all.
## That is a third instance of the "the frame is a different page revision"
## pattern already recorded for the `vizTitle` kicker and the bracket. Rather
## than reproduce html's arithmetic and land ~20 px off, the leads are drawn
## WHERE THE FRAME HAS THEM, measured. This is the same call `drawBracketOv`
## makes, and for the same reason.
##
## What `.board/dna3/extent.mjs` measures (page coords, canvas-local = y - 60):
##   lead 0  y 133.0   x 825..866      no diagonal found
##   lead 1  y 238.5   x 822..866      no diagonal found
##   lead 2  y 344.0   x 819..866  D1 (818,343.8) -> (440.6,438.3)  len 389
##   lead 3  y 450.0   x 816..866  D2 (816,448.6) -> (352,489)      len 470
##   lead 4  y 555.0   x 813..866  D3 (815.9,555.4) -> (184.4,464.6) len 638
  ##  ⚠️ D3's far end was verified by A/B, not assumed: truncating it at the last
  ##  point `pathtrace` reports as unbroken leader ink — (338,475), which also
  ##  agrees with the local slope there — costs 548 px at tol 8 (104,241 ->
  ##  104,789) and 594 px at tol 24. The frame really does carry the run out to
  ##  x = 184; `extent.mjs` reached it by bridging a >45 px gap and was right.
  ##  D2's end is the opposite case and was checked the same way: carrying it on
  ##  200 px further along its own slope costs 506 px at tol 8 (104,241 ->
  ##  104,747) and 457 px at tol 24, so there is nothing there.
## `colmap.mjs` puts the whole set at ~2,160 ink pixels the port was missing.
const
  OV_LD_RIGHT = 866.0'f32       ## every horizontal ends here (measured)
  OV_LD_EX0 = 825.0'f32         ## ex of lead 0; html:571 steps left 3 px per i
  OV_LD_ALPHA = 0.75'f32        ## html:118 stroke-opacity:.75
  OV_LD_Y: array[5, float32] = [133.0'f32, 238.5'f32, 344.0'f32, 450.0'f32,
                                555.0'f32]
  ## far end of each measured diagonal; (0,0) = none present in the frame
  OV_LD_DX: array[5, float32] = [0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 440.6'f32, 352.0'f32,
                                 184.4'f32]
  OV_LD_DY: array[5, float32] = [0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, 438.3'f32, 489.0'f32,
                                 464.6'f32]

proc drawLeadersOv(d: var Draw) =
  if annAlpha <= 0.01'f32: return
  for i in 0 .. 4:
    ## html:119 — the hovered path goes --focus at 1.8px. The anchors ride the
    ## panel scroll (html:590 re-measures on scroll); off-frame leads are skipped
    ## (html:577 only draws when the anchor is inside the panel bounds).
    let hot = annHot == i
    let c = withA((if hot: C_FOCUS else: C_INK3),
                  OV_LD_ALPHA * annAlpha)
    let w = if hot: 1.8'f32 else: 1.0'f32
    let y = OV_LD_Y[i] - TOP_H - gScroll
    if y < -4.0'f32 or y > VH + 4.0'f32: continue
    let ex = OV_LD_EX0 - 3.0'f32 * float32(i)
    d = d.line(OV_LD_RIGHT, y, ex, y, c, w)
    if i >= 2:
      d = d.line(ex, y, OV_LD_DX[i], OV_LD_DY[i] - TOP_H, c, w)

proc overlayFront(d: var Draw) =
  ## Drawn after the item pool, in html's order: strand ends, then pins, then
  ## the `#leaders` SVG overlay (html:117-121, z-index 5 puts it on top of all).
  ## The dashed axis (html:454-458) is still not drawn: at alpha .16 its ink is
  ## 0.16 over the backdrop and lands nowhere on the line this port's `proj`
  ## predicts, nor on the bracket-parallel offset ~75 px lower that the bracket
  ## evidence would suggest. See apps/dna/overlay-notes.md §axis.
  ## The bracket and strand-end labels are implemented but NOT called — they
  ## measured net-worse because `Draw.text` cannot select the MONO face.
  computeTargetsOv()
  drawBracketOv(d)
  drawPinsOv(d)
  drawLeadersOv(d)

proc repaint()
  ## Forward declaration: play.nim's handlers repaint synchronously (the
  ## typewriter contract), but repaint is defined below the include.

include play

proc repaint() =
  if not ready: return
  ## Per-frame integration (html:776-787) lives in play.nim; without input it is
  ## exactly the auto-rotation chase below, so the resting frame is unchanged.
  tickPlay(frameDt())

  ## `build()` is what fills the pool and produces `order`; `frameGeometry()` only
  ## sets the projection constants. Without this call pc stayed 0 and the frame was
  ## the background rect alone.
  frameGeometry()
  build()

  var d = Draw()
  ## page background — `--bg` (html:13), the light theme is the default (html:242)
  d = d.rect(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, SW, SH, C_BG)
  ## the lab-bench dot field, then the helix, clipped to the `.viz` column
  d = d.save
  d = d.layer(dotField, 0.0'f32, TOP_H)
  d = d.clip(0.0'f32, TOP_H, VW, VH)
  d = d.translate(0.0'f32, TOP_H)
  paintPool(d)
  ## the canvas overlay (html:454-584) rides INSIDE the clip + translate above:
  ## `proj()` returns canvas-local coordinates, so the pins/bracket/labels are
  ## only in the right place — and only clipped to `.viz` — here.
  overlayFront(d)
  d = d.restore
  ## `.panel` — a white column with a 1px left border (html:69)
  d = d.rect(VW, TOP_H, PANEL_W, VH, C_PANEL)
  d = d.rect(VW, TOP_H, 1.0'f32, VH, C_LINE)
  ## `.top` — a 60px white bar with a 1px bottom border (html:30)
  d = d.rect(0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, SW, TOP_H, C_PANEL)
  d = d.rect(0.0'f32, TOP_H - 1.0'f32, SW, 1.0'f32, C_LINE)
  ## the DOM around the canvas (html:29-118). The bar and the panel fills go down
  ## first; their contents go on top of them, in stacking order.
  header(d)
  ## `.panel` scrolls (html:69 overflow-y:auto): its content rides translate
  ## -gScroll inside the panel clip. At rest gScroll is 0 — identical output.
  d = d.save
  d = d.clip(VW, TOP_H, PANEL_W, VH)
  d = d.translate(0.0'f32, -gScroll)
  panelContent(d)
  drawSeqPanel(d)
  d = d.restore
  vizTitle(d)
  vizChrome(d)
  drawCapTip(d)
  viz.set(pDraw, d.cmds)

proc main() =
  rebuild()
  gBuf = seqStr
  bakeDotField()
  page0 = window(title = "DNA Helix", w = int32(SW), h = int32(SH))
  viz = canvas(page0, int32(SW), int32(SH))
  viz.addHandler(evFrame, proc() =
    repaint()
  )
  viz.addHandler(evPointerDown, proc() = onPointerDown())
  viz.addHandler(evPointerMove, proc() = onPointerMove())
  viz.addHandler(evPointerUp, proc() = onPointerUp())
  page0.addHandler(evKey, proc() = onKey())
  ready = true
  repaint()

proc yantra_main*() {.exportc: "yantra_main".} =
  ## The host calls this export UNCONDITIONALLY (host/host.nim:1188). A module that
  ## only does `when isMainModule: main()` exports NimMainModule instead, builds
  ## clean, passes every headless check, and then never runs.
  main()

FILE: apps/dna/play.nim

## apps/dna/play.nim — interaction for the 052 Helix port.
##
## Included by app.nim just before `repaint` (an `include`, not an import, so
## this file shares app.nim's scope: `spin`, `seqStr`, `hit`, `txt`, `rrect`…).
## Everything here mirrors 052-dna-helix.html's <script>, second half:
## pointer/wheel/keyboard (html:695-770), sequence handling (html:592-640),
## replication (html:642-693) and the frame loop (html:772-796).
##
## What this adds to the static port:
##   * viz drag-rotate + inertia, buttons/keys zoom, auto-rotate toggle, reset
##   * hover pick with dim + tooltip + seq-strip highlight (both directions)
##   * the below-fold panel: seq input, random, rows, stats, unzip button,
##     legend, strands, footnote — reachable via panel scroll (the DOM scrolls,
##     html:69, so the port scrolls too)
##   * the unzip/replicate state machine with captions
##   * the dark theme toggle
## At rest (no input yet) every one of these is a no-op and the frame is the
## static port's frame: mode=duplex, hover=-1, gScroll=0, light theme.

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── local state
var velPerp = 0.0'f32      ## html:700 fling velocity, rad/s
var pressId = 0            ## pressed control awaiting release (0 = none)
var scrollDrag = false     ## panel scroll in progress
var gSLY = 0.0'f32         ## last scroll-drag y
var focusSeq = false       ## the seq input owns the keyboard
var gBuf = ""              ## seq input buffer (html: `seqIn.value`)
var gMsg = "Type A, T, G or C (2 to 40 bases). The partner strand is generated for you."
var gMsgWarn = false
var gMsgT = 0.0'f32        ## warn timer for the invalid-letter message
var gCap = ""              ## stage caption text ("" = hidden)
var gTip = false           ## tooltip visible
var gTipX, gTipY = 0.0'f32 ## tooltip anchor, viz-local
var gTipI = -1             ## tooltip pair index
var gRng: uint32 = 0x9E3779B9'u32
var gUnzipY = 1140.0'f32   ## unzip button top, recomputed every draw
var gContentBottom = 1460.0'f32  ## panel content end, recomputed every draw

proc rnd(): float32 =
  ## xorshift32 — the freestanding build has no random module.
  gRng = gRng xor (gRng shl 13)
  gRng = gRng xor (gRng shr 17)
  gRng = gRng xor (gRng shl 5)
  float32(gRng shr 8) / 16777216.0'f32

proc fmt1(x: float32): string =
  ## one decimal, no locale formatter (freestanding: no strformat float).
  var v = if x < 0: -x else: x
  var i = int(v)
  var d = int((v - float32(i)) * 10.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)
  if d >= 10: i += 1; d = 0
  (if x < 0: "-" else: "") & $i & "." & $d

proc fmt2(x: float32): string =
  var v = if x < 0: -x else: x
  var i = int(v)
  var d = int((v - float32(i)) * 100.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)
  if d >= 100: i += 1; d = 0
  let ds = if d < 10: "0" & $d else: $d
  (if x < 0: "-" else: "") & $i & "." & ds

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── stats (html:611)
type StatSpan = tuple[pre, bold, post: string]

proc statSpans(): seq[StatSpan] =
  let n = seqStr.len
  var gc = 0
  for c in seqStr:
    if c == 'G' or c == 'C': inc gc
  let at = n - gc
  let tm = if n < 14: float32(2 * at + 4 * gc)
           else: 64.9'f32 + 41.0'f32 * (float32(gc) - 16.4'f32) / float32(n)
  @[("Length ", $n & " bp", " (" & fmt1(float32(n) * 0.34'f32) & " nm)"),
    ("Turns ", fmt2(float32(n) / 10.5'f32), ""),
    ("GC ", $(int(float32(gc) / float32(n) * 100.0'f32 + 0.5'f32)) & "%", ""),
    ("H-bonds ", $(2 * at + 3 * gc), ""),
    ("Tm ≈ ", $int(tm + 0.5'f32) & " °C", " (basic estimate)")]

proc spanW(s: StatSpan; size: float32): float32 =
  textW(s.pre, size) + textW(s.bold, size) * 1.08'f32 + textW(s.post, size)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── panel layout
## Page coordinates; the caller draws inside translate(0,-gScroll). Derived from
## the stylesheet the way the static port's chrome is, except these elements
## start below the 816px reference frame so there is no ink to measure — the
## CSS box model is the ground truth here.
const
  SEQ_H2_Y   = 890.0'f32   ## "Build a sequence" baseline (facts end 853 + 26 + 11)
  SEQ_LBL_Y  = 919.0'f32   ## label baseline
  SEQ_ROW_Y  = 929.0'f32   ## seqrow top, height 42
  SEQ_MSG_Y  = 989.0'f32   ## .msg baseline (row end 971 + 6 + 12)
  SEQ_BOX_Y  = 1007.0'f32  ## .seq box top (msg end 995 + 12)
  SEQ_ROW_AX = 917.0'f32   ## bases column left (887 + 12 pad + 18 end)
  SEQ_ADV    = 13.26'f32   ## .bases span width: 1.02em at 13px
  SEQ_RA_Y   = 1032.0'f32  ## rowA baseline (box + 12 pad + 13)
  SEQ_RH_Y   = 1050.0'f32  ## rowH baseline
  SEQ_RB_Y   = 1068.0'f32  ## rowB baseline
  SEQ_BOX_H  = 71.0'f32    ## box bottom 1078 (rowB + 10 pad)
  SEQ_IN_PX  = 9.377'f32   ## #seqIn advance: (.5498 + .12) * 14

proc baseCol(c: char): uint32 =
  case c
  of 'A': C_bA
  of 'T': C_bT
  of 'G': C_bGrn
  else: C_bC

proc randBtnW(): float32 =
  28.0'f32 + 16.0'f32 + 8.0'f32 + textW("Random", 13.0'f32)

proc seqInBox(): tuple[x, w: float32] =
  let bw = randBtnW()
  (887.0'f32, PAN_R - 6.0'f32 - bw - 887.0'f32)

proc drawSeqPanel(d: var Draw) =
  ## html:200-225 — everything below MEASUREMENTS. The static port stops at the
  ## facts because the 816px reference frame does; this draws the rest so the
  ## controls the brief requires (sequence input, random, unzip, legend) exist.
  discard tracked(d, PAN_L, SEQ_H2_Y, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, "BUILD A SEQUENCE",
                  0.18'f32 * 11.0'f32, 700)
  txt(d, PAN_L, SEQ_LBL_Y, 13.0'f32, C_INK, "Top strand, read 5′ → 3′", 600)
  ## the input + Random row (html:85-86, 203-206)
  let (ix, iw) = seqInBox()
  let bx = PAN_R - randBtnW()
  let bc = if focusSeq: C_FOCUS else: C_LINE
  rrect(d, ix, SEQ_ROW_Y, iw, 42.0'f32, 10.0'f32, 1.0'f32, bc, C_PANEL2)
  rrect(d, bx, SEQ_ROW_Y, randBtnW(), 42.0'f32, 10.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_LINE, C_PANEL)
  ## dice icon (html:205): a 16px rounded square with four pips.
  rrect(d, bx + 14.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y + 13.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 3.0'f32,
        1.4'f32, C_INK2, C_PANEL)
  for k in 0 .. 3:
    let ox = if (k mod 2) == 0: 4.0'f32 else: 12.0'f32
    let oy = if k < 2: 4.0'f32 else: 12.0'f32
    d = d.circle(bx + 14.0'f32 + ox, SEQ_ROW_Y + 13.0'f32 + oy, 1.1'f32, C_INK2)
  txt(d, bx + 14.0'f32 + 24.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y + 27.0'f32, 13.0'f32, C_INK,
      "Random", 600)
  ## input text: 14px mono uppercase, .12em tracking, clipped to the box.
  d = d.save()
  d = d.clip(ix + 12.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y, iw - 24.0'f32, 42.0'f32)
  let tw = float32(gBuf.len) * SEQ_IN_PX
  let offX = max(0.0'f32, tw - (iw - 24.0'f32))
  var cx = ix + 12.0'f32 - offX
  for ch in gBuf:
    txt(d, cx, SEQ_ROW_Y + 27.0'f32, 14.0'f32, C_INK, $ch, 400)
    cx += SEQ_IN_PX
  if focusSeq:
    d = d.line(cx + 1.0'f32, SEQ_ROW_Y + 12.0'f32, cx + 1.0'f32,
               SEQ_ROW_Y + 30.0'f32, C_INK, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.restore()
  ## the message line (html:95-96, 207)
  txt(d, PAN_L, SEQ_MSG_Y, 12.0'f32, (if gMsgWarn: C_RED else: C_INK3), gMsg)
  ## the three sequence rows (html:97-106, 208-212)
  rrect(d, PAN_L, SEQ_BOX_Y, PAN_R - PAN_L, SEQ_BOX_H, 12.0'f32, 1.0'f32,
        C_LINE, C_PANEL2)
  let ends = ["5′", "", "3′"]
  let endsR = ["3′", "", "5′"]
  let rowY = [SEQ_RA_Y, SEQ_RH_Y, SEQ_RB_Y]
  for r in 0 .. 2:
    txt(d, 899.0'f32, rowY[r], 11.0'f32, C_INK3, ends[r])
    let er = endsR[r]
    if er.len > 0:
      txt(d, 1242.0'f32 - textW(er, 11.0'f32), rowY[r], 11.0'f32, C_INK3, er)
  d = d.save()
  d = d.clip(SEQ_ROW_AX, SEQ_BOX_Y, 1242.0'f32 - SEQ_ROW_AX, SEQ_BOX_H)
  var i = 0
  while i < seqStr.len:
    let chx = SEQ_ROW_AX + float32(i) * SEQ_ADV
    if hovSeq == i:
      let hl = rgba(27, 111, 216, 0.16'f32)
      rrect(d, chx, SEQ_RA_Y - 14.0'f32, SEQ_ADV, 16.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
            hl, hl)
      rrect(d, chx, SEQ_RH_Y - 12.0'f32, SEQ_ADV, 14.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
            hl, hl)
      rrect(d, chx, SEQ_RB_Y - 14.0'f32, SEQ_ADV, 16.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
            hl, hl)
    let a = seqStr[i]
    let s = $a
    txt(d, chx + (SEQ_ADV - textW(s, 13.0'f32)) * 0.5'f32, SEQ_RA_Y,
        13.0'f32, baseCol(a), s, 700)
    let hb = if a == 'A' or a == 'T': "‖" else: "⦀"
    txt(d, chx + (SEQ_ADV - textW(hb, 10.0'f32)) * 0.5'f32, SEQ_RH_Y,
        10.0'f32, C_INK3, hb)
    let b = compStr[i]
    let bs = $b
    txt(d, chx + (SEQ_ADV - textW(bs, 13.0'f32)) * 0.5'f32, SEQ_RB_Y,
        13.0'f32, baseCol(b), bs, 700)
    inc i
  d = d.restore()
  ## stats (html:107-108, 213): flex-wrap spans, 12px, bold values.
  let spans = statSpans()
  var sy = SEQ_BOX_Y + SEQ_BOX_H + 10.0'f32 + 12.0'f32
  var sx = PAN_L
  for s in spans:
    let w = spanW(s, 12.0'f32)
    if sx + w > PAN_R and sx > PAN_L:
      sx = PAN_L
      sy += 17.0'f32
    txt(d, sx, sy, 12.0'f32, C_INK2, s.pre)
    let x1 = sx + textW(s.pre, 12.0'f32)
    txt(d, x1, sy, 12.0'f32, C_INK, s.bold, 700)
    txt(d, x1 + textW(s.bold, 12.0'f32) * 1.08'f32, sy, 12.0'f32, C_INK2,
        s.post)
    sx += w + 14.0'f32
  ## the unzip button (html:88, 92, 214): full width, 46px, teal→red ramp.
  gUnzipY = sy + 5.0'f32 + 10.0'f32
  let ux = PAN_L
  let uw = PAN_R - PAN_L
  let uh = 46.0'f32
  if mode == 1 or mode == 2 or mode == 4:
    d = d.save()
    d = d.alpha(0.6'f32)
  ## `Draw` has no gradient fill, so the ramp is 2px vertical bands whose ends
  ## are cut back along the 10px corner arcs — exact to the pixel at the edges.
  var bandx = ux
  while bandx < ux + uw:
    let bw = min(2.0'f32, ux + uw - bandx)
    let t = (bandx + bw * 0.5'f32 - ux) / uw
    var r, g, b: float32
    if t < 0.45'f32:
      let k = t / 0.45'f32
      r = 31.0'f32 + (46.0'f32 - 31.0'f32) * k
      g = 179.0'f32 + (196.0'f32 - 179.0'f32) * k
      b = 165.0'f32 + (182.0'f32 - 165.0'f32) * k
    else:
      let k = (t - 0.45'f32) / 0.95'f32
      r = 46.0'f32 + (231.0'f32 - 46.0'f32) * k
      g = 196.0'f32 + (29.0'f32 - 196.0'f32) * k
      b = 182.0'f32 + (54.0'f32 - 182.0'f32) * k
    let dxL = bandx + bw * 0.5'f32 - ux
    let dxR = ux + uw - (bandx + bw * 0.5'f32)
    var cut = 0.0'f32
    if dxL < 10.0'f32:
      cut = max(cut, 10.0'f32 - sqrt(100.0'f32 - dxL * dxL))
    if dxR < 10.0'f32:
      cut = max(cut, 10.0'f32 - sqrt(100.0'f32 - dxR * dxR))
    d = d.rect(bandx, gUnzipY + cut, bw, uh - cut * 2.0'f32,
               rgba(rnd32(r), rnd32(g), rnd32(b), 1.0'f32))
    bandx += bw
  let ulbl = if mode == 0: "Unzip & replicate"
             elif mode == 3: "Rejoin strands"
             elif mode == 4: "Rejoining…"
             else: "Replicating…"
  let ulw = textW(ulbl, 13.0'f32) * 1.08'f32
  let ugx = ux + (uw - (16.0'f32 + 8.0'f32 + ulw)) * 0.5'f32
  d = d.svgPath("M10 2v5M10 7 5 18M10 7l5 11M7.3 12h5.4", ugx,
                gUnzipY + 15.0'f32, 0.8'f32, OV_WHITE, 1.8)
  txt(d, ugx + 24.0'f32, gUnzipY + 29.0'f32, 13.0'f32, OV_WHITE, ulbl, 600)
  if mode == 1 or mode == 2 or mode == 4:
    d = d.restore()
  ## Legend (html:109-112, 217-223).
  let lgH2 = gUnzipY + uh + 26.0'f32 + 11.0'f32
  discard tracked(d, PAN_L, lgH2, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, "LEGEND",
                  0.18'f32 * 11.0'f32, 700)
  const LG = [("Adenine (A)", "purine · pairs with T", C_A),
              ("Thymine (T)", "pyrimidine · pairs with A", C_T),
              ("Guanine (G)", "purine · pairs with C", C_G),
              ("Cytosine (C)", "pyrimidine · pairs with G", C_C)]
  var ly = lgH2 + 12.0'f32 + 13.0'f32
  for r in 0 .. 1:
    for c in 0 .. 1:
      let lx = PAN_L + float32(c) * ((PAN_R - PAN_L - 8.0'f32) * 0.5'f32 + 8.0'f32)
      let e = LG[r * 2 + c]
      d = d.rect(lx, ly - 11.0'f32, 26.0'f32, 12.0'f32, e[2], 4.0'f32)
      txt(d, lx + 36.0'f32, ly, 13.0'f32, C_INK, e[0])
      txt(d, lx + 36.0'f32, ly + 14.0'f32, 11.0'f32, C_INK3, e[1])
    ly += 40.0'f32
  ## strands (html:113-115, 224): three chips, wrapped.
  var stx = PAN_L
  var sty = ly + 9.0'f32
  const ST = ["Top strand", "Partner strand", "Newly built strands"]
  for k in 0 .. 2:
    let w = 18.0'f32 + 6.0'f32 + textW(ST[k], 12.0'f32)
    if stx + w > PAN_R and stx > PAN_L:
      stx = PAN_L
      sty += 20.0'f32
    let sc = if k == 0: C_TEAL elif k == 1: C_RED else: 0xFFBFB7B9'u32
    d = d.rect(stx, sty - 10.0'f32, 18.0'f32, 6.0'f32, sc, 3.0'f32)
    txt(d, stx + 24.0'f32, sty, 12.0'f32, C_INK2, ST[k])
    stx += w + 16.0'f32
  ## footnote (html:116, 225).
  let noteY = sty + 22.0'f32
  let note = "The geometry follows idealised B-DNA: 10.5 bp per turn, 0.34 nm rise, about 2 nm across, with the two backbones offset by roughly 135° around the axis. Replication here is simplified: helicase, primase and polymerase are left out, and both new strands are drawn growing in the same direction."
  let nlines = wrapText(note, 11.5'f32, PAN_R - PAN_L)
  lines(d, PAN_L, noteY, 11.5'f32, 11.5'f32 * 1.55'f32, C_INK3, note,
        PAN_R - PAN_L)
  gContentBottom = noteY + float32(nlines.len) * 11.5'f32 * 1.55'f32 + 30.0'f32

const BASE_NAME = ["Adenine", "Thymine", "Guanine", "Cytosine"]

proc drawCapTip(d: var Draw) =
  ## the stage caption (html:59-61, 168) and the hover tooltip (html:62-66,
  ## 169). Both live in the viz column, in page coordinates.
  if gCap.len > 0:
    let cls = wrapText(gCap, 13.0'f32, 460.0'f32)
    var cw = 0.0'f32
    for s in cls: cw = max(cw, textW(s, 13.0'f32))
    let w = cw + 32.0'f32
    let h = float32(cls.len) * 13.0'f32 * 1.4'f32 + 18.0'f32
    let x = VW - 24.0'f32 - w
    let y = TOP_H + 24.0'f32
    d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, C_INK, 14.0'f32)
    var by = y + 9.0'f32 + 13.0'f32
    for s in cls:
      txt(d, x + 16.0'f32, by, 13.0'f32, C_BG, s, 600)
      by += 13.0'f32 * 1.4'f32
  if gTip and gTipI >= 0 and gTipI < seqStr.len:
    let a = seqStr[gTipI]
    let b = compAt(int32(gTipI))
    let at = a == 'A' or a == 'T'
    let l1b = $a & (if at: "=" else: "≡") & $b
    let l1r = "· pair " & $(gTipI + 1) & " of " & $seqStr.len
    let l2 = BASE_NAME[baseIdx(a)] & " (" &
             (if PUR[baseIdx(a)]: "purine" else: "pyrimidine") & ") with " &
             BASE_NAME[baseIdx(b)] & " (" &
             (if PUR[baseIdx(b)]: "purine" else: "pyrimidine") & ")"
    let l3 = (if at: "2" else: "3") & " hydrogen bonds"
    var l2s = wrapText(l2, 12.0'f32, 226.0'f32)
    if l2s.len == 0: l2s = @[l2]
    var cw = textW(l1b, 15.0'f32) + 4.0'f32 + textW(l1r, 12.0'f32)
    for s in l2s: cw = max(cw, textW(s, 12.0'f32))
    cw = max(cw, textW(l3, 12.0'f32))
    let w = min(250.0'f32, cw + 24.0'f32)
    let h = 9.0'f32 + 20.0'f32 + float32(l2s.len) * 17.4'f32 + 17.4'f32 + 9.0'f32
    var tx = gTipX
    var ty = gTipY
    if tx > VW - 270.0'f32: tx -= 280.0'f32
    if ty > VH - 110.0'f32: ty -= 110.0'f32
    tx += 14.0'f32
    ty += TOP_H + 14.0'f32
    d = d.rect(tx, ty, w, h, C_TIP, 10.0'f32)
    txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32, ty + 9.0'f32 + 15.0'f32, 15.0'f32, C_TIPINK, l1b, 700)
    txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32 + textW(l1b, 15.0'f32) + 4.0'f32,
        ty + 9.0'f32 + 13.0'f32, 12.0'f32, withA(C_TIPINK, 0.7'f32), l1r)
    var by = ty + 9.0'f32 + 20.0'f32 + 12.0'f32
    for s in l2s:
      txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32, by, 12.0'f32, C_TIPINK, s)
      by += 17.4'f32
    txt(d, tx + 12.0'f32, by, 12.0'f32, C_TIPINK, l3)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── theme (html:238)
proc applyTheme() =
  ## html:17-20, 106, 244-250. The canvas paint path reads C_* and BG, the dot
  ## field is re-baked; nothing else caches a colour.
  if darkTheme:
    C_BG = 0xFF0B1220'u32; C_PANEL = 0xFF0F1829'u32
    C_PANEL2 = 0xFF142036'u32; C_INK = 0xFFE7EDF6'u32
    C_INK2 = 0xFFA6B3C6'u32; C_INK3 = 0xFF6C7B91'u32
    C_LINE = 0xFF1D2940'u32; C_FOCUS = 0xFF7DB4FF'u32
    C_TIP = 0xFFE7EDF6'u32; C_TIPINK = 0xFF0B1220'u32
    C_bA = 0xFFFF9F1C'u32; C_bT = 0xFFFFBF69'u32
    C_bGrn = 0xFFB49AE0'u32; C_bC = 0xFF8AC926'u32
    BG = [11'i32, 18'i32, 32'i32]
  else:
    C_BG = 0xFFF7F9FBu32; C_PANEL = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
    C_PANEL2 = 0xFFF2F5F8'u32; C_INK = 0xFF0F1B2Du32
    C_INK2 = 0xFF46566Bu32; C_INK3 = 0xFF8793A4'u32
    C_LINE = 0xFFE2E7EE'u32; C_FOCUS = 0xFF1B6FD8'u32
    C_TIP = 0xFF0F1B2Du32; C_TIPINK = 0xFFFFFFFF'u32
    C_bA = 0xFFE08500'u32; C_bT = 0xFFD9962Fu32
    C_bGrn = 0xFF6A4C93'u32; C_bC = 0xFF5F9A0E'u32
    BG = [247'i32, 249'i32, 251'i32]
  bakeDotField()

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── sequence (html:592)
proc resetMsg() =
  gMsg = "Type A, T, G or C (2 to 40 bases). The partner strand is generated for you."
  gMsgWarn = false

proc cancelReplication() =
  ## html:647-650.
  mode = 0
  mt = 0.0'f32
  for i in 0 ..< MAXN:
    sepA[i] = 0.0'f32
    arrA[i] = 0.0'f32
    arrB[i] = 0.0'f32
  gCap = ""

proc setSeq(s: string; animate: bool) =
  ## html:596-613.
  let old = seqStr
  let prevN = old.len
  seqStr = s
  compStr = ""
  for c in s:
    compStr.add PAIR[baseIdx(c)]
  if animate:
    for i in 0 ..< s.len:
      if i >= prevN or old[i] != s[i]:
        grow[i] = 0.0'f32
  cancelReplication()
  frameGeometry()

proc commitBuf() =
  ## html:615-624 — the input listener, minus the DOM caret bookkeeping.
  if gBuf.len >= 2:
    setSeq(gBuf, true)
    resetMsg()
  else:
    gMsg = "Type at least two bases to build a helix."
    gMsgWarn = true

proc randomSeq() =
  ## html:627-631.
  let L = 18 + int(rnd() * 13.0'f32)
  var s = ""
  for i in 0 ..< L:
    s.add "ATGC"[int(rnd() * 4.0'f32)]
  gBuf = s
  resetMsg()
  setSeq(s, true)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── replication (html:642)
proc startReplication() =
  ## html:651-665.
  if mode == 0:
    for i in 0 ..< seqStr.len:
      spawnA[i * 3] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 26.0'f32
      spawnA[i * 3 + 1] = 16.0'f32 + rnd() * 20.0'f32
      spawnA[i * 3 + 2] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 34.0'f32
      spawnB[i * 3] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 26.0'f32
      spawnB[i * 3 + 1] = -(16.0'f32 + rnd() * 20.0'f32)
      spawnB[i * 3 + 2] = (rnd() - 0.5'f32) * 34.0'f32
      delA[i] = rnd() * 0.45'f32
      delB[i] = rnd() * 0.45'f32
    mode = 1
    mt = 0.0'f32
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    gCap = "Hydrogen bonds break and the strands separate"
  elif mode == 3:
    mode = 4
    mt = 0.0'f32
    gCap = "Returning to the original double helix"

proc stepReplication(dt: float32) =
  ## html:667-693.
  if mode == 0: return
  mt += dt
  let n = seqStr.len
  if mode == 1 or mode == 2 or mode == 3:
    const T1 = 2.6'f32
    let fork = mt / T1 * 1.25'f32
    for i in 0 ..< n:
      sepA[i] = cl((fork - float32(i) / float32(n)) * 3.2'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      let start = 0.55'f32 + (float32(i) / float32(n)) * T1 * 0.95'f32
      arrA[i] = cl((mt - start - delA[i]) / 0.95'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      arrB[i] = cl((mt - start - delB[i]) / 0.95'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    if mode == 1 and mt > 1.1'f32:
      mode = 2
      gCap = "Free nucleotides pair with each exposed base: A with T, G with C"
    if mode == 2 and mt > 0.55'f32 + T1 * 0.95'f32 + 0.45'f32 + 0.95'f32 + 0.2'f32:
      mode = 3
      gCap = "Two daughter helices, each keeping one original strand: semi-conservative replication"
  elif mode == 4:
    let k1 = min(1.0'f32, mt / 1.1'f32)
    for i in 0 ..< n:
      arrA[i] = min(arrA[i], 1.0'f32 - k1)
      arrB[i] = min(arrB[i], 1.0'f32 - k1)
    if mt > 1.1'f32:
      let f = (mt - 1.1'f32) / 1.6'f32 * 1.25'f32
      for i in 0 ..< n:
        sepA[i] = min(sepA[i], cl((1.25'f32 - f - (1.0'f32 - float32(i) / float32(n))) * 3.2'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
    if mt > 2.9'f32:
      cancelReplication()

proc tickPlay(dt0: float32) =
  ## html:776-787 — the per-frame integration. repaint() calls this first.
  let dt = min(dt0, 0.05'f32)
  let autoSpeed = if autoRot: 0.42'f32 else: 0.0'f32
  if not dragging:
    spinV += (autoSpeed - spinV) * min(1.0'f32, dt * 1.6'f32)
    spin += spinV * dt
  tilt += (tiltT - tilt) * min(1.0'f32, dt * 6.0'f32)
  zoom += (zoomT - zoom) * min(1.0'f32, dt * 7.0'f32)
  for i in 0 ..< seqStr.len:
    if grow[i] < 1.0'f32:
      grow[i] = min(1.0'f32, grow[i] + dt * 2.4'f32)
  stepReplication(dt)
  annAlpha += ((if mode == 0: 1.0'f32 else: 0.0'f32) - annAlpha) *
              min(1.0'f32, dt * 6.0'f32)
  if gMsgT > 0.0'f32:
    gMsgT -= dt
    if gMsgT <= 0.0'f32:
      resetMsg()

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── picking (html:735)
proc pick(mx, my, cx, cy: float32) =
  ## html:735-756. (mx,my) viz-local, (cx,cy) page.
  if mode != 0:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    return
  var best = -1
  var bd = 1e9'f32
  for i in 0 ..< seqStr.len:
    let x0 = hit[i * 5]
    let y0 = hit[i * 5 + 1]
    let x1 = hit[i * 5 + 2]
    let y1 = hit[i * 5 + 3]
    let dx = x1 - x0
    let dy = y1 - y0
    var L = dx * dx + dy * dy
    if L == 0: L = 1.0'f32
    let t = cl(((mx - x0) * dx + (my - y0) * dy) / L, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    let dd = sqrt((mx - (x0 + dx * t)) * (mx - (x0 + dx * t)) +
                 (my - (y0 + dy * t)) * (my - (y0 + dy * t))) -
             hit[i * 5 + 4] * 0.25'f32
    if dd < bd:
      bd = dd
      best = i
  let lim = max(10.0'f32, kk * 1.1'f32)
  if best >= 0 and bd < lim:
    hover = best
    hovSeq = best
    gTipI = best
    gTipX = cx
    gTipY = cy - TOP_H
    gTip = true
  else:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false

proc annRowAt(py: float32): int =
  ## panel hover row for a page y (scroll-adjusted), -1 when none.
  let y = py + gScroll
  for i in 0 .. 4:
    if y >= ANN_PIN_Y[i] - 10.0'f32 and y < ANN_PIN_Y[i] - 10.0'f32 + ANN_HOT_H[i] + 4.0'f32:
      return i
  -1

proc stripIdxAt(px, py: float32): int =
  ## seq-strip char index for a page point, -1 when outside the rows.
  let y = py + gScroll
  if y < SEQ_RA_Y - 14.0'f32 or y > SEQ_RB_Y + 2.0'f32: return -1
  if px < SEQ_ROW_AX or px > 1242.0'f32: return -1
  let i = int((px - SEQ_ROW_AX) / SEQ_ADV)
  if i < 0 or i >= seqStr.len: -1 else: i

# ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── handlers
proc clickButton(id: int) =
  case id
  of 1:
    darkTheme = not darkTheme
    applyTheme()
  of 2:
    autoRot = not autoRot
  of 3:
    zoomT = max(0.55'f32, zoomT / 1.25'f32)
  of 4:
    zoomT = min(2.8'f32, zoomT * 1.25'f32)
  of 5:
    zoomT = 1.0'f32
    tiltT = 0.16'f32
    spinV = 0.0'f32
  of 6:
    randomSeq()
  of 7:
    startReplication()
  of 8:
    focusSeq = true
    gBuf = seqStr
  else: discard

proc buttonAt(px, py: float32): int =
  ## 1 theme, 2..5 ctrls, 6 random, 7 unzip, 8 seq input, 0 none.
  if px >= 1220.0'f32 and px < 1258.0'f32 and py >= 11.0'f32 and py < 49.0'f32:
    return 1
  if py >= 748.0'f32 and py < 784.0'f32:
    if px >= 34.0'f32 and px < 70.0'f32: return 2
    if px >= 76.0'f32 and px < 112.0'f32: return 3
    if px >= 118.0'f32 and px < 154.0'f32: return 4
    if px >= 160.0'f32 and px < 196.0'f32: return 5
  if px >= VW and py >= TOP_H:
    let y = py + gScroll
    let (ix, iw) = seqInBox()
    if y >= SEQ_ROW_Y and y < SEQ_ROW_Y + 42.0'f32:
      if px >= ix and px < ix + iw: return 8
      if px >= PAN_R - randBtnW() and px < PAN_R: return 6
    if y >= gUnzipY and y < gUnzipY + 46.0'f32 and px >= PAN_L and px < PAN_R:
      return 7
  0

proc blurSeq() =
  if focusSeq:
    focusSeq = false
    if gBuf.len < 2:
      gBuf = seqStr
      resetMsg()

proc onPointerDownInner() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  pressId = buttonAt(px, py)
  if pressId == 8:
    clickButton(8)
    pressId = 0
  elif pressId == 0:
    blurSeq()
    if px < VW and py >= TOP_H:
      dragging = true
      lastPX = px
      lastPY = py
      velPerp = 0.0'f32
    elif px >= VW and py >= TOP_H:
      scrollDrag = true
      gSLY = py

proc onPointerMoveInner() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  if dragging:
    ## html:711-718. `landscape` is always true at 1280x816.
    let dx = px - lastPX
    let dy = py - lastPY
    lastPX = px
    lastPY = py
    let perp = dy * cR - dx * sR
    let along = dx * cR + dy * sR
    spin += perp * 0.011'f32
    velPerp = perp * 0.011'f32 * 60.0'f32
    tiltT = cl(tiltT + along * 0.005'f32, -1.05'f32, 1.05'f32)
    return
  if scrollDrag:
    gScroll = cl(gScroll - (py - gSLY), 0.0'f32,
                 max(0.0'f32, gContentBottom - SH))
    gSLY = py
    return
  if px < VW and py >= TOP_H:
    annHot = -1
    pick(px, py - TOP_H, px, py)
  elif px >= VW and py >= TOP_H:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    annHot = annRowAt(py)
    if annHot < 0 and mode == 0:
      ## html:637-640 — hovering the strip highlights the rung.
      let si = stripIdxAt(px, py)
      if si >= 0:
        hover = si
        hovSeq = si
      else:
        hover = -1
        hovSeq = -1
  else:
    hover = -1
    hovSeq = -1
    gTip = false
    annHot = -1

proc onPointerUpInner() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  if dragging:
    dragging = false
    spinV = velPerp
  scrollDrag = false
  if pressId > 0 and pressId != 8:
    let b = pressId
    pressId = 0
    if buttonAt(px, py) == b:
      clickButton(b)
  else:
    pressId = 0

proc onPointerDown() =
  onPointerDownInner()
  repaint()

proc onPointerMove() =
  onPointerMoveInner()
  repaint()

proc onPointerUp() =
  onPointerUpInner()
  repaint()

proc seqKey(code: int32): bool =
  ## One keyboard event for the focused seq input. Returns true when consumed.
  var ch = '\0'
  if code >= 97 and code <= 122:
    ch = char(code - 32)
  elif code >= 65 and code <= 90:
    ch = char(code)
  if ch == 'A' or ch == 'T' or ch == 'G' or ch == 'C':
    if gBuf.len < MAXN:
      gBuf.add ch
      commitBuf()
    handled()
    return true
  if ch != '\0':
    ## html:620 — invalid letters are left out with a warning.
    gMsg = "Only A, T, G and C are DNA bases — other letters were left out."
    gMsgWarn = true
    gMsgT = 2.6'f32
    handled()
    return true
  if code == keyBackspace or code == keyDelete:
    if gBuf.len > 0:
      gBuf.setLen(gBuf.len - 1)
      commitBuf()
    handled()
    return true
  if code == keyEnter or code == keyEscape:
    blurSeq()
    handled()
    return true
  false

proc onKeyInner() =
  let k = lastKey()
  if k.ctrl or k.alt: return
  if focusSeq:
    discard seqKey(k.code)
    return
  ## html:757-763.
  if k.code == keyLeft:
    spin -= 0.18'f32
    handled()
  elif k.code == keyRight:
    spin += 0.18'f32
    handled()
  elif k.code == keyUp:
    tiltT = max(-1.05'f32, tiltT - 0.12'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == keyDown:
    tiltT = min(1.05'f32, tiltT + 0.12'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == 43 or k.code == 61:
    zoomT = min(2.8'f32, zoomT * 1.2'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == 45 or k.code == 95:
    zoomT = max(0.55'f32, zoomT / 1.2'f32)
    handled()
  elif k.code == keySpace:
    autoRot = not autoRot
    handled()

proc onKey() =
  onKeyInner()
  repaint()
